Amasomo 3

Umwandiko: Rugigana n’ingoma ye
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 149)

Intera ya mbere: Gusoma no kumva umwandiko
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusoma umwandiko yubahiriza utwatuzo n’iyitsa.
– Gusobanura amagambo akomeye ari mu mwandiko.
– Kwitabira gukoresha neza mu mvugo no mu nyandiko amagambo yungutse.
– Gusubiza ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri kirimo umwandiko w’inkuru
ndende ku bukoroni, igitabo cy’umwarimu, amashusho mvumburamatsiko
n’inkoranyamagambo.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwitegereza amashusho ari mu gitabo
cy’umunyeshuri ahari umwandiko bagiye gusoma ababaze ibibazo bibafasha
kumva icyo asobanura no kuvumbura ibikubiye mu mwandiko yifashishije
ayo mashusho. Usibye ayo mashusho yo mu gitabo, umwarimu ashobora no
kwifashisha izindi mfashanyigisho zifatika nk’ibiboko abaporisi bitwaza cyangwa
andi mashusho ajyanye n’umwandiko akabaza ibibazo biganisha ku mwandiko
bagiye gusoma

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza aramutse akoresheje amashusho:
a) Ni iki mubona kuri aya mashusho? Turahabona umwami, abantu bafite
amacumu n’abazungu.
b) Ku bwanyu murumva ari iki cyabaye? Turumva umwami yakiriye abazungu
ibwami.
c) Ukeka ko bariya bazungu ari ba nde? Nkeka ko ari abakoroni b’i Burayi.
Nyuma yo gusubiza ibyo bibazo cyangwa ibindi yababaza biganisha ku mwandiko
bagiye kwiga, umwarimu arahishurira abanyeshuri ko bagiye gusoma umwandiko
uvuga ku bukoroni.

220

2. Gusoma umwandiko
2.1. Gusoma bucece

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kurambura igitabo cyabo ahari umwandiko
“Rugigana n’ingoma ye”, abasabe kuwusoma bucece nyuma ababaze ibibazo
byo gusuzuma ko basomye.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Muri uyu mwandiko baratubwira ko ubukoroni ari iki?Ni ubutegetsi
bw’igihugu kigize igihangange kinjira mu kindi kikiha kukiyobora.
b) Inkoni abakoroni bakubitishaga yitwa ngo iki? Ni ikiboko, indembo.
c) Imirimo y’uburetwa yakorwaga ku bw’abakoroni bayitaga ngo iki? Shiku.
d) Umwami w’u Rwanda utarigeze yumvikana n’abazungu ni nde? Ni Yuhi V
Musinga.

2.2. Gusoma baranguruye

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko wose baranguruye.
Umunyeshuri umwumwe arajya asoma igika kimwe, abandi bakurikira mu
bitabo byabo. Nyuma yo kurangiza igika umwarimu asaba undi munyeshuri
kumusimbura bityo bityo kugeza umwandiko wose urangiye. Mu gihe umwarimu
ashakisha umunyeshuri usoma, arajya anagenzura uko bitabira gushaka gusoma.
Mu gihe basoma aragenda abakosora aho basomye nabi amagambo cyangwa aho
batubahiriza neza utwatuzo.

2.3. Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda

Barangije gusoma baranguruye, umwarimu ashyira abanyeshuri mu matsinda
anyuranye. Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri bagize buri tsinda kwishakamo umwe
ujya wandika ibisubizo bumvikanyeho muri buri tsinda. Akanabasaba gusoma
umwandiko no gushakishiriza hamwe ibisobanuro by’amagambo badasobanukiwe
kandi basubiza n’ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Umwarimu agabanya amatsinda ibibazo biri mu bitabo byabo, itsinda rimwe
rigafata ibibazo bibiri cyangwa bitatu bitewe n’umubare w’abanyeshuri
n’uw’amatsinda yakoze.
Umwarimu agendagenda mu matsinda agenzura uko abanyeshuri bakorera
hamwe, agatanga ubufasha ku babumusabye.
Nyuma y’iki gikorwa, umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kumurika ibyavuye muri
buri tsinda.
Umwarimu akabafasha kunonosora ibisubizo byavuye mu matsinda anyuranye,
bakabyandika ku kibaho no mu makayi yabo.

221

I. Inyunguramagambo
Urugero rw’ibisubizo bishobora kuvamu matsinda.

(a) Sobanura amagambo akurikira:

Gukomeza umutsi: kwihangana.
Guhara amagara: kwiyemeza gukora ikintu ukaba wanakizira.
Amagambo azaba ashize ivuga: byose bizaba birangiye.
Amagi y’umuzungu: amagi abaturage bagemuriraga Ababirigi baraye ku gasozi
runaka.
5. Inka z’umugogoro: ni inka zakamirwaga umuzungu waraye ku gasozi kariho
abatunzi, bashoboraga gukama zahumuza bakazicyura, bitashoboka bakiyemeza
kujya bagemura.
6. Inka ya Rugaravu: umubare w’inka zabagirwaga abasirikari b’Abanyekongo
n’abakozi ba Leta n’amasosiyete nka SOMUKI yacukuraga i Rutongo,
GEORWANDA y’i Rwinkwavu na MINETE (MINETAIN) yacukuraga i Bugarama
n’i Musha n’ahandi.
7. Amajune: ibyago, ibibazo, akaga.
8. Ngisimbuka akatsi: nta cyo ngishoboye, singishoboye kugenda, ndashaje.
9. Urushingo: agati k’umuko bakoreshaga bashaka umuriro.
10. Urushingati: agati k’umuko bakubagamo urushingo kugira ngo haboneke
umuriro.
11. Batadukoma mu nkokora: batatubangamira
12. Kubyinira ku rukoma: kwishima cyane, kwizihirwa, kumva utuje.
13. Mukabiva imuzingo: mukabisobanura bihagije.
14. Ubutegetsi bwa kiboko: ubutegetsi bukandamiza, ubutegetsi bukoresha igitugu.
15. Kugenda mpiru na nyoni: kugenda burundu ntugaruke.
16. Kugenda nka kabindi na buki: kugenda ugahera.
17. Ubuvivi n’ubuvivure: abana bakomoka ku gisekuru k’inyuma cyane, uhereye
ku mwana w’umwuzukuruza wawe.

b) Umwitozo w’inyunguramagambo

Umwarimu abwira kujya mu matsinda ya babiribabiri kugira ngo bakore umwitozo uri
mu bitabo byabo. Abagenera igihe bagomba gukora uwo mwitozo, icyo gihe cyarangira
akabayobora maze bagakosorera hamwe uwo mwitozo. Umwe mu bagize itsinda
rimwe ajya imbere akavuga yandika ku kibaho ibyo itsinda ryabo ryakoze, nyuma
umwarimu n’abandi banyeshuri bakanonosora ibisubizo byatanzwe bikandikwa ku
kibaho no mu makayi. Ibyo bisubizo kandi babisoma mu ijwi riranguruye kugira ngo
n’abatabona ku kibaho babyumve.

ii) Simbuza ijambo ritsindagiye irindi bivuga kimwe dusanga mu
mwandiko.

1. Kazungu/umukoroni/Gashakabuhake yaratuzengereje duta umutwe kugeza
ku bwigenge, ubu turaho.
2. Jya wiga ushishikaye, ntukamere nka za nkorabusa zijya ku ishuri ari ukurangiza
umuhango.
3. I Bwotamasimbi haturutseyo Abakoroni b’ingeri zose.
4. Bantu mukinisha imyuko isohora amashaza muge mwitonda mutazatumaraho
urubyaro.
5. Abamaze kubona igufwa ry’umuyaga, bazi byinshi ku mateka y’Abakoroni
bigabagabanyije Afurika.
6. Kugera ku bwigenge byarwaniwe inkundura.
7. Ururimi rwacu gakondo ntirwigeze rwitabwaho mu mashuri ku ngoma ya
Gashakabuhake w’umukoroni.

II. Ibibazo n’ibisubizo byo kumva umwandiko

1. Abakinankuru bavugwa mu mwandiko ni ba nde? Ruhakana Gamariyeli,
Nyirashiku Rasheli, Rumashana Shabani, Turikubwigenge Natanayeli.
2. Uruhinja rw’umukoroni rwari rukomeye. Tanga interuro iri mu mwandiko
ibihamya. Ngo nta mutware kabeba. uruhinja rw’umukoroni rwashoboraga
kugukoresha icyo rushatse kandi ururusha amaboko.
3. Mu mwandiko baratubwiramo umusaza ukuze cyane. Uwo ni nde,
umubwirwa n’iki? Umusaza uvugwa ni Rumashana. Yuzuye iminkanyari mu
gahanga, umusatsi wose ni uruyenzi; yarwanye iy’Abamayimayi n’Abamawumawu
aharanira ko Abakoroni badakomeza kudutsikamira no kudupyinagaza.
4. Abakoroni bafite ibintu bibiri by’ingenzi bitwaje bakoroniza Afurika. Ibyo
bintu ni ibihe? Kwigisha ijambo ry’Imana, guhagarika ubucuruzi bw’abacakara.
5. Ingaruka mbi kurusha izindi za gikoronize yashegeshe igihugu cyacu cy’u
Rwanda ni iyihe? Ingaruka mbi ni Jenoside Yakorewe Abatutsi muri Mata 1994.
6. Vuga imitwe ibiri y’abarwanyi yari yarazengereje Abakoroni mu bihugu
by’iburasirazuba bw’u Rwanda. Iyo mitwe y’abarwanyi ni Abamawumawu muri
Kenya n’Abamayimayi mu magepfo ya Tanzaniya.
7. Inama yo kwigabanya Afurika yateranye kandi irangira ryari? Hehe? Yari
iyobowe na nde? Mu nama yateranye ku wa 15 Ugushyingo 1884 irangira ku wa
26 Gashyantare, 1885 i Berilini (Berlin) mu Budage iyobowe n’ uwitwa Oto Voni
Bisimariki (Otto voni Bismarck).
8. Ubwo u Rwanda rwatandukanywaga n’u Burundi na Kongo rwatakaje
ahantu hanini. Havuge. U Rwanda rwatakaje icya gatatu cy’ubutaka bwarwo.
Ijwi, Bwishya, Gishari, na Gisigari babyometse kuri Kongo; Abongereza bikubira
Bufumbira na Ndorwa-Mpororo babitwerera intara ya Kigezi n’iya Nkole. U
Rwanda rwavaga aha rukarenga ibirunga mu majyaruguru, rugasingira ibiyaga
bihari ugakomeza ugafata Kampala y’ubu ari wo murwa mukuru w’u Bugande,

224

aho twarahatakaje; mu burengerazuba, u Rwanda rwafataga za Butembo, Masisi,
Bukavu, Nyamwirima na za Katanga, aho ubu ni muri “Repubulika Iharanira
Demukarasi ya Kongo”.
9. Tanga amazina y’imigi yashinzwe n’Abakoroni n’abo yitiriwe. Twavuga Kongo
Burazavile yashinzwe na Savorunya do Buraza (Savorgnan De Brazza), Kigali yo
mu Rwanda ishingwa na Rishari Kanti (Richard Kandt)...

Intera ya kabiri: Gusesengura umwandiko
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 156)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusubiza neza ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko.
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba ashobora gusobanura ibyiza
n’ingaruka z’ubukoroni.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri n’igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
zivuga ku bukoroni.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo bigamije kubibutsa umwandiko baheruka
gusoma bakavuga muri make n’ibyari biwukubiyemo.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Ni uwuhe mwandiko duheruka gusoma? Rugigana n’ingoma ye!
b) Wavugaga kuki muri rusange? Wavugaga cyanecyane ku ngaruka mbi zatewe
n’ubukoroni.
Nyuma yo gusubiza ibyo bibazo, umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri kurambura ibitabo
byabo ahari umwandiko “Rugigana n’ingoma ye”.

2. Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda no kuwusesengura

Umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko bari mu matsinda no
kugerageza kuwusesengura bagaragaza insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko
n’ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwamo. Umwarimu araha abanyeshuri ibibazo bibafasha
kuvumbura insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko n’ibindi bituma bagaragaza
ingingo z’ingenzi ziri mu mwandiko. Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko
arabivuga mu ijwi riranguruye anabyandike ku kibaho kugira ngo afashe abatumva
niba bahari.

225

Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko n’ibisubizo:
1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu nkuru? Insanganyamatsiko ivugwamo ni
ubukoroni.
2. Tanga ingingo z’ingenzi zibanzweho mu nkuru wasomye.
– Ukwigabagabanya Afurika.
– Ibihugu byigabanyije Afurika.
– Ubutaka ibihugu by’afurika byatakaje.
– Intambara zo kwigobotora Abakoroni.
– Ubuhemu bw’abakoroni.
3. Abakoroni badusigiye ibikomere n’agashashi katumurikira kujya mbere. Sobanura
byimbitse.
Agashashi katumurikira kujya imbere:
– Abakoroni bashinze amashuri bigisha gusoma no kwandika.
– Basize bimwe mu bihingwa ngengabukungu.
– Bazanye ifaranga.
– Bazanye idini rishingiye ku kwemera Yezu Kirisitu.
Ibikomere:
– Batubibyemo amacakubiri.
– Basahuye imitungo y’ibihugu bakoronije.
– […]
4. Ingamba ababyiruka bagomba gufata kugira ngo hakomeze gusigasira
ibyagezweho nyuma yo kwiyuha akuya ni izihe? Bagomba kwiga bashyizeho
umwete, bagomba kwivuza mu mavuriro akomeye, mu yandi magambo bagomba
kumva ko bagomba kujijuka bagakomeza gukumira icyatuma Afurika yongera
guhungabana, by’umwihariko mu Rwanda rwacu.

Intera ya gatatu: Kungurana ibitekerezo
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasesenguye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Kugereranya ubuzima abamo n’ibivugwa mu nkuru.
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba agaragaza imyitwarire yo kurwanya
amacakubiri mu bantu.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
zivuga ku bukoroni.

226

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu arabaza abanyeshuri ibibazo bigamije kwibutsa isomo baheruka
kwiga.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yabaza:
a) Ni irihe somo duheruka kwiga? Duheruka gusesengura umwandiko uvuga ku
bukoroni.
b) Uwo mwandiko wasorezaga ku yihe ngingo y’ingenzi? Wasorezaga ku ngingo
ivuga ku buhemu bw’abakoroni.

2. Kungurana ibitekerezo

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo
ugaragara mu gitabo cy’umunyeshuri aranabasaba kuwandika ku kibaho kugira
ngo afashe abatumva neza kuwubona no kuwusobanukirwa.

Umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo

“Nimugereranye ibivugwa mu nkuru n’ubuzima murimo muri iki gihe.”
Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwishakamo umuyobozi w’ikiganiro
nyunguranabitekerezo. Uwo arajya aha abandi umwanya wo gutanga ibitekerezo
byabo no kugira icyo yavuga ku bitekerezo by’abandi mu bwubahane. Mu gihe
bungurana ibitekerezo umwarimu agenzura uko abanyeshuri bitabira gutanga
ibitekerezo kugira ngo abatabyitabira abibashishikarize. Nyuma yo kungurana
ibitekerezo umwarimu afatanya n’abanyeshuri gukora umwanzuro ku byo
bunguranyeho ibitekerezo. Bikandikwa ku kibaho.

Uko wakosorwa

Umwarimu arareba niba mu mwanzuro wafashwe abanyeshuri bagaragaza ko
Abanyarwanda muri iki gihe bayobowe neza kandi bakaba bishimiye ubuyobozi ariko
ko mu mwandiko havugwagamo abaturage batishimiye ubukoroni bwabayobozaga
igitugu.

227

Intera ya kane: Inkuru ndende
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasesenguye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gutanga inshoza y’inkuru ndende.
– Gusobanura ibiranga inkuru ndende.
– Kuvuga amateka y’inkuru ndende.
– Kugena ingeri z’inkuru ndende nyarwanda ahereye ku ngingo zazo.
– Gusesengura inkuru ndende yasomye.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’igitabo
cy’ubuvanganzo nyarwanda.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu arasaba abanyeshuri kongera gusoma umwandiko “Rugigana n’ingoma
ye” ababaze ibibazo bigamije gutahura ubwoko bwawo.

Urugero rw’ibibazo n’ibisubizo:
Murabona uwo mwandiko utangira ute? Uratangira ugaragaza ko hari ibindi
byawubanjirije bitagaragajwe.
Ese iyo muwusomye mwumva urangiye? Twumva utarangiye, ahubwo hari ibikibura
umuntu yakongeraho.
Murakeka ko ari bwoko ki? Turabona ari inkuru ndende.

2. Isesengura

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kujya mu matsinda ya banebane akabasaba
gusoma imwe mu nkuru ndende no gukora ubushakashatsi ku ngeri y’inkuru
ndende. Abarangira ibitabo bakwifashisha mu nzu y’isomero akabaha igihe cyo
kubikora. Abaha ibibazo bibayobora mu bushakashatsi bwabo, igihe cyo kumurika
ibyakorewe mu matsinda cyagera akabafasha kubinonosora bikandikwa ku kibaho
no mu makayi yabo.

Urugero rw’inkuru ndende basoma:
- Munyakazi, S., Ntabajyana, 1952
- Rukebesha, A., Nyirabirahunga, 1970
- Nsanzubuhoro, V., Ntabyera, 1971
- Kamugunga, C., Umusiramu, 1973
- Karege F., Mwanankundi, 1975

228

-

Byuma, F., Mureranyana, 1981
Niyonteze, P., Imari ya shuni, 1981-1982
Uwamungu, J., Nyirabayazana, 1981
Nkurikiyumukiza, F., Yatashye atagomba, 1987
Niyitegeka, M.Y., Giramata, 1988
............................, Urwibutso, 1988
............................, Wishavura, 1989
............................, Iyo mbimenya, 1991
Rugema, A., Rwemerikije, 1988
Furere, R.M., Mariya Kantarama, 1998
Karenzi, F., Ishavu ry’abato, 2000
...

Ibibazo by’ubushakashatsi:
–
–
–
–

Inkuru ndende ni iki? Irangwa n’iki?
Vuga amateka yayo.
Sobanura ingeri z’inkuru ndende nyarwanda.
Iyo basesengura inkuru ndende bibanda ku ki?

Urugero rw’ibyava mu bushakashatsi:

a) Inshoza y’inkuru ndende

Inkuru ndende nk’uko iryo zina ribivuga ni inkuru iba ari ndende, ibarwa
n’umubarankuru avuga uko yagenze. Bamwe mu basesenguzi b’inkuru ndende
bayivuga berekana ko igomba kuvuga ibyabayeho ndetse umwanditsi akavuga
ubuzima bwe; ibyamubayeho. Abandi bati: “Igomba kuba ari inkuru y’impimbano
n’ubwo ibyo ivuga byashobora kubaho.” Igihurirwaho na benshi ni uko inkuru
ndende igomba kuba ifite inkuru ibara, uruhererekane rw’ibikorwa, ikaba
yanditse mu nyandiko isanzwe; atari mu mikarago nk’ibisigo kandi yifitemo
ubwiza n’ubuhanga bw’imikoreshereze y’ururimi. Ibi, babishimangira bagira bati:
“Inkuru ndende ni uruhererekane rw’ibikorwa bihimbano bishobora kubaho
cyangwa byabayeho, ikaba ifite imisusire ya gihanga kandi nyabugeni igaragaza ko
umwanditsi ari intyoza mu kubara inkuru, mu kuyiha imiterere myiza y’ibikorwa
no kubikurikiranya.”

b) Uturango tw’inkuru ndende

Inkuru ndende irangwa n’imiterere yayo; ibarankuru, abakinankuru (abantu
cyangwa inyamaswa), kuba hari ikivugwa, kuba hari ahantu n’igihe kibera, kuba
ari ndende (akenshi hagati y’impapuro 100 na 250) kandi ifite ingingo nyinshi
kuba hari uburyo ibikorwa bikurikirana kandi bigenda bitera amatsiko usoma,
ku buryo atarambirwa gusoma inkuru ibarwa.

c) Amateka y’inkuru ndende mu Rwanda

Hari abavuga ko inkuru ndende mu Rwanda yaba yaratangiye mu mwaka wa 1938
kuko ari bwo Musenyeri Alexis Kagame yatangiye gusohora inyandiko ze «Inkuru

229

ya Matabaro» mu kanyamateka k’abana Hobe. Abashakashatsi bemeza ariko ko
inkuru ndende nyayo yasohotse mu wa 1950 yanditse mu rurimi rw’Igifaransa na
Saveriyo Nayigiziki yise «Escapade rwandaise» ifite ibice bibiri byaje guhurizwa
mu nkuru imwe ikitwa «Mes trances à trente ans». Hashize imyaka ibiri nibwo
inkuru ndende yanditse mu Kinyarwanda yasohotse yitwa «Ntabajyana» ya
Simoni Munyakazi. Uyu mugabo yahawe igihembo cya kabiri mu irushannwa
ryiswe «Amitiés Belgo-Rwandaise». Kuva ubwo haciye imyaka igera kuri 20 kugira
ngo haboneke izindi nkuru ndende mu irushanwa ryabaye mu wa 1971.

d) Amoko y’inkuru ndende nyarwanda

Iyo bagena ingeri z’inkuru ndende bazishyira mu matsinda, hari uburyo bwinshi
bukurikizwa. Ibihurirwaho na benshi bagena amoko y’inkuru ndende ni ibi
bikurikira : aho yandikiwe, igihe yandikiwe cyangwa se ikivugwamo, ibarankuru
ryayo (imiterere y’ibikorwa, abakinnyi) n’ibindi. Izi zikurikira ni zimwe mu ngeri
z’inkuru ndende nk’uko Nkejabahizi Jean Chrisostome azigena mu gitabo ke
“Ubuvanganzo nyarwanda. Inkuru ndende n’Ikinamico, Butare UNR, 2005”

1. Inkuru ndende z’inkundo

Zikunda kuvuga cyane ku nkundo hagati y’abasore n’inkumi, ingorane zishobora
kubatandukanya cyangwa se kubabuza kubana, kwitsinda no kudahemukirana
mu bigeragezo, amayeri yose akoreshwa kugira ngo bahure cyangwa basubirane
mu gihe ababyeyi babo batabishaka, amaherezo bakazagera ku ntego yabo yo
kubana. Mu Kinyarwanda, aha hakunze gutangwa ingero za Ntabajyana ya Simoni
Munyakazi na Giramata ya Niyitegeka Mukarugira Yuliyana.

2. Inkuru ndende nsuhuzamutima

Bene izi nkuru zamamaye cyane mu mpera z’ikinyejana cya cumi na gatandatu no
mu ntangiriro z’icya cumi na karindwi. Inkuru iba ishingiye ahanini ku bwihare;
bahera ku gashashi cyangwa akanyotwe k’urukundo gasanzwe, kagashyigikirwa
n’ibikorwa bibiri by’ingenzi: ingendo n’imirwano. Urukundo ruvugwamo
ruriyubashye, ni isugi kandi ntiruhemuka. Umukundwa cyangwa uwihebewe
usanga ari agakumi. Ibizazane bahura na byo usanga ari ibishobora kubaho mu
buzima cyanecyane nk’igihe k’imidugararo. Kudahuza idini bituma ababyeyi
bivayo n’imizi n’imiganda ngo babuze umusore n’inkumi kubana. Ikemezo cya se
w’umukobwa cyo kumushyingira uwo ashatse kubera inyungu ze bwite, ubukire,
gutandukanywa n’intambara cyangwa se gufungwa. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero
twatanga hano ni Ntabajyana ya Simoni Munyakazi kubera ko ababyeyi ba
Karasankima batifuzaga ko arongora Zaninka, ahubwo bafite undi bamuhitiyemo
wo mu rwego rwabo, ariko umusore Karasankima akababera ibamba.

3. Inkuru ndende z’imyifatire

Izi ni inkuru zishingiye ku busongarere n’ubugome mu maraso. Imibereho ya
buri munsi n’imiterere y’isi muri icyo gihe, ntibishyigikirwa n’umuco muzima
wo kwiyubaha n’ubuhanga byaranze abanditsi bakomeye. Mu nkuru ndende

230

nk’izi usanga higanjemo gushimisha irari ry’umubiri, kuba ikigenge(ubwomanzi),
gutinyuka gukabije, ibiterasoni ; mbese usanga isi yaracuramye ku buryo ikibi
kiganje, akaba ari cyo kiyobora isi. Muri ubu bwoko bw’inkuru, usanga imico myiza
n’ubupfura byitwa ubugwari, ubucucu n’amakosa. Muri izo nkuru usangamo
amagambo atameshe, umwanditsi akihatira gushushanya mu mvugo ibifitanye
isano n’ibitsina, aho bibera n’uburyo atanga ingingo ze byerekana ko ari indwara
imaze igihe. Mu Kinyarwanda nta rugero rwa bene izi nkuru ruraboneka kugeza
ubu.

4. Inkuru ndende za sarigoma

Inkuru nk’izi zifite amavuko yazo mu gihugu cya Espagne (Esipanye) mu kinyejana
cya cumi na karindwi. Uruhare runini ruhabwa imibereho mu by’ubukungu :
kugira icumbi, kubona ifunguro, umwambaro ni byo bihora bihangayikishije
abanyarubuga muri ubu bwoko bw’inkuru. Iyo nkuru bayitiriye uburyo ibazemo,
kuko ari inkuru y’umuntu uvuga ubuzima bwe. Byabaho bitabaho, umubarankuru
mukuru avuga ubuzima n’imibereho ye.
Imyandikire y’iyi nkuru bayihuza n’imibereho y’umwana ubaho ari mbonabucya, nta
cyo yimirije imbere, nta gashinga k’ubuzima afite. Mu buzima bw’umubarankuru
nyuma y’igihe runaka k’imibabaro n’ibibazo, hakurikiraho igihe cyo kwiyuburura
no gutwarwa agakundwa, ubukire yifuzaga bukaza mu bitekerezo ariko atabwizera,
ugasanga aritiranya inzozi n’ukuri. Inkuru ndende nyarwanda ifite aho ihuriye
na bene ubu bwoko ni iya Saveriyo Nayigiziki yitwa «Mes trances à trente ans»

5. Inkuru ndende y’ubuzima busanzwe

Bene iyi nkuru ntiba igamije gukosora ngo wenda ibintu byarushaho kuba byiza,
abantu bagire imyifatire iboneye, babane neza, mbese ngo ubone ko umwanditsi
afite inzozi z’umunezero n’ubuhehemure. Iyi nkuru irangwa n’urusobe cyangwa
uruvangitirane rw’imyifatire, kuticara hamwe, gusetsa, gusesereza, uburara
n’ubwomanzi no kwifatira abantu. Iritegereza, igakabya mu gusetsa, igakabya
inkuru n’ubucakura, hagaragaramo ibitangaza no kwimaringa. Uzasanga iyi
nkuru ivuga ukuntu runaka yakoze kugira ngo yirwaneho, kugira ngo akomere
abone amaboko n’amafaranga; uko yagiriye nabi abanzi be, uko yatsinzwe
cyangwa se yatsinze mu nkiko, uko yaje guteseka agashakisha uburyo yagana
imigi itandukanye; uko yaje guhinduka umugiranabi agakora n’andi makosa
bigatuma bamufunga; ibikorwa bibi yagizemo uruhare, kwiyoberanya, ingendo,
abo bahura n’ibindi. Inkuru y’Ikinyarwanda y’ubu bwoko ni iya FranÇois Byuma
yitwa Mureranyana.

6. Inkuru ndende za mutemberezi/ naragenze ndabona

Bakunze kuvugamo ubwiza bw’abantu, ibidukikije. Babivuga ari nk’umutako w’aho
abakinankuru baba bari ku buryo bishushanya uko bamerewe imbere muri bo.

231

Ari abantu, ari inyamaswa, usanga ari mahwi, biberanye n’aho biri bisa n’aho ari
ho byaremewe na ho hakabiremerwa. Ahantu ni ho hatuma abakinnyi batekereza
gutya na gutya, ni ho hababeshejeho ku buryo ubuzima bwabo bwajyanaga n’uko
isi ibakikije iteye. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero dutanga ni Imari ya SHUNI ya Pascal
Niyonteze.

7. Inkuru ndende barwa

Ubwoko bw’iyi nkuru bwaje kwemerwa mu kinyejana cya cumi n’umunani.
Havugwamo agahinda no kubona ko ibyo wibeshyaga mu nzozi, wibwira ko
ari ko bizamera nyamara ko bitagishobotse. Umuntu uvugwamo arashaka
ariko ntashobore. Ubushobozi iyo bumubanye buke bituma abeshya cyangwa
se akiyerekana uko bitari ubundi yiyerekanaga nk’umunyakuri, maze uwari
yararahiye kudasa na rubanda rusanzwe akagenda akajya inyuma y’abandi mu
ngeso mbi z’urukozasoni. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero ni “Iyo mbimenya” ya
Niyitegeka Mukarugira Yuliyana.

8. Inkuru za nanzubukoroni

Iyi nkuru igaragaza umujinya no kuzinukwa kw’abantu bahinduwe abacakara,
bakoronijwe n’abazungu, maze kwivumbura kwabo kukagira ingaruka kuri bose
ndetse n’umuntu ku giti ke. Izi nkuru zakunze kugaragara mu myaka ya za mirongo
itandatu, ubwo ibihugu bya Afurika byahagurukiraga rimwe bigamije kwipakurura
ubutegetsi bwa gikoroni. Izi nkuru zigaragaza akarengane, gusuzugurwa no
guteshwa agaciro Abanyafurika bagirirwaga. Urugero twatanga mu Kinyarwanda
ni Mureranyana ya FranÇois Byuma nubwo yo itavuga gusa ikibazo cy’ubukoroni
na “Matabaro” ya Alegisi kagame.

9. Inkuru ndende ya subiza amerwe mu isaho

Abanyafurika bamaze kwigobotora ingoma ya gikoronize bari bazi ko bagiye
kwigenga no kumererwa neza, ibyo bari barabuze bakabibona ntibongere
gusuzugurwa no gufatwa nabi, kwicishwa imirimo n’ibindi. Bagize batya babona
ntacyahindutse. Abashyizwe ku ngoma y’ubutegetsi nta ho bigeze batandukanira
n’abakoroni, ndetse bamwe mu bategetsi b’Abanyafurika barushije ubugome
abitwaga abakoroni. Abaturage barumiwe amerwe bayasubiza mu isaho.
Abategetsi banyunyuje abaturage, barabakandagira karahava: akarengane,
gufungirwa ubusa, kwigira mu migi rubanda igasigara iririra mu myotsi y’icyaro,
ahatagira amazi n’umuriro, nta terefone, inzara n’ubukene binuma. Abategetsi
baradamarara, rubanda bicuza icyo barwaniye imyaka n’imyaka ngo barashaka
kwigenga, ku buryo hari n’abasigara bifuza ko bwa butegetsi bw’abakoroni
barwanyaga bwagaruka. Urugero rwo mu Kinyarwanda usanga rujya kwegera
ubu bwoko ni usanga ruberanye n’ubu bwoko bw’inkuru ni Mureranyana ya
FranÇois Byuma.

232

10. Inkuru ndende z’amateka

Ubwoko bw’izi nkuru bushingira ku bantu bagize uruhare mu mateka y’aha
n’aha, umuryango, igihugu; bakavuga uko babayeho, ibyo bakoze n’ibindi. Muri
Afurika twavuga nka Caka (Chaka), Sunjata (Soundjata), n’abandi. Bene izi
nkuru zitandukanye n’inkuru ndende nyirizina. Mu nkuru z’amateka usangamo
ibitekerezo bivanze n’amakabyankuru. Inkuru y’Ikinyarwanda ivuga ku mateka
ni Mureranyana ya FranÇois Byuma.

11. Inkuru ndende z’intimba

Zitekereza ku buzima n’imibereho, zitwereka ko kubaho ari ugushinyiriza, ko
ubuzima atari paradizo. Kubaho ni ukubabara. Muri izi nkuru usanga umuntu
akunda ntakundwe, yahinga akarumbya, yakira agahangayika. Iyo agerageje
kwipfira nabi cyangwa kwishabikira uko abishoboye bitewe n’uko isi yameze
amenyo, arinda apfa agikururana n’umuruho. Igihe umuntu akiriho yumva ko
ikiruta ari ukwipfira akava ku isi kuko n’ubundi asanga ari yo maherezo. Muri
izi nkuru usanga umuntu abura aho apfunda imitwe, aho agannye hose asanga
amaherere yamutanze imbere akifuza ko nyamunsi yaza ikamwanzuranya akigira
kwa Nyamutezi atagumye kugaragurika mu ruzurungutane rw’ibibazo bidashira.
Inkuru nyarwanda yenda kwegera ubu bwoko bw’inkuru ni Yatashye atagomba
ya Phocas Nkurikiyumukiza.

12. Inkuru ndende z’uburere

Kubura icyo ufata n’icyo ureka byaranze Abanyafurika b’igisekuru cyose cya
makumyabiri, barerewe mu mico n’imigenzereze y’i Burayi kubera amashuri,
bagahuza ubwenge n’amaso n’imigi minini nka Parisi (Paris), Londoni (Londres),
bakabangamirwa no kwibaza ukuntu bazatahuka iwabo bagasubira mu mwijima,
mu bukene, mu bujiji,…ngibyo ibyo ahenshi inkuru z’uburere zuririraho, aho
usanga umukinankuru yabuze icyo afata n’icyo areka hagati y’imico mishyashya
yasanze aho yagiye n’ibya gakondo yakuriyemo, maze kubivanga bikamuviringa
ubwenge, abazi kuvuga neza bati: « Naka yasarishijwe n’ubwenge ». Iyo usesenguye
neza, usanga mu Rwanda nta nkuru ndende dusanga iri neza muri uyu murongo,
ariko hari izigaragaza iryo sizana ry’ibya kera n’iby’ubu. Urugero rutangwa ni nka
Mwanankundi ya Karege Fidèle aho avugamo umunyarubuga Mwanankundi
wagiye kwiga i Burayi ibyerekeranye n’imibereho myiza n’imibanire y’abantu.
Aho Mwanankundi atahukiye yashatse kuvuganira abagore no kubarengera
imbere y’umuco wabakandamizaga, wa kera ushaje, abatsimbaraye ku bya kera
baramwivuganye ngo arabatobera.

13. Inkuru ndende nshyashya

Izi nkuru zadutse nyuma y’intambara ya kabiri y’isi yose. Mbere yaho, inkuru
ndende yasaga n’iyubakiye ku muntu wari warahawe agaciro gakomeye, yenda

233

tutavuze kuva akiremwa, ariko byibuze mu rwego rw’ubuvanganzo, mu mpumeko
y’ibyo bise ivukabushya « renaissance » mu kinyejana cya cumi na gatanu, bamaze
kuvumbura ibyo gucapisha imashini, umuco n’ubugeni bigahabwa agaciro na
muntu agasa n’uhinduka impagata y’ibyaremwe byose.
Aho intambara zombi z’isi zibereye bakabona ukuntu umuntu ateshwa agaciro,
agasigara arutwa n’ibintu, bamwe batangiye kwibaza niba mu buvanganzo ho
bitahinduka, muntu ntiyongere kuba ari we uhabwa umwanya w’ibanze aho
byose usanga ari we bigushaho : abakinnyi, kuranga, ishushanyamvugo n’ibindi.
Inkuru ndende nshyashya nta kindi bisobanura uretse guhuza abanditsi bose
bashakashaka imiterere mishya y’inkuru ndende zashobora kuvuga cyangwa se
kurema amasano mashya hagati y’umuntu n’isi, ni ukuvuga abiyemeje guhanga
inkuru ndende ari byo kurema. Muri iki gihe usanga inkuru nshyashya isa nk’aho
itigeze ishinga imizi. Iyi nkuru ntishingira ku mukinankuru w’imena nk’uko byari
bimenyerewe. Umwanditsi aha ijambo abantu benshi kandi muri ngenga ya mbere.
Ntumenya uvuga uwo ari we, umubazi w’inkuru arazimira.
Mu nkuru nshyashya, nta nsanganyamatsiko, nta kureshya umusomyi baca
igikuba. Abari bamenyereye ibya kera barayirwanyije karahava, bakavuga ko
atari ukuri ndetse ko ari igisa n’ubuvanganzo kubera ko yanze kuba basabose.
Mu nkuru nshyashya ubona gusa uruhererekane rw’amagambo anyuranye ndetse
yivuguruza, ibitekerezo bituzuye, mbese ibintu by’ikivangavange nk’uko isi yari
imeze ikiremwa. Kugeza ubu mu Kinyarwanda nta rugero rw’inkuru nshyashya
ruraboneka.

14. Inkuru ndende porisi

Inkuru ndende porisi yubakiye ku kuvumbura ubuhanga kandi buhorobuhoro
wifashishije uburyo busanzwe, uko ibintu byagenze ku kintu kidasanzwe cyabaye.
Abashakashatsi n’abanditsi benshi bahuriza hamwe ko inkuru ndende porisi
ari iperereza rikorwa ku buryo busanzwe cyangwa se bwa gihanga. Iri perereza
riba rigamije kuvumbura, guhishura ikintu cyayoberanye, kitumvikana, cyabaye
imenamutwe. Mu magambo avunaguye inkuru ndende porisi ni inkuru y’umuhigo
wa muntu, bakoresheje gutekereza ku tuntu wakwita amafuti bakatubyaza
igisubizo.
Uko inkuru porisi ikura :
– Habanza insanganyamatsiko igizwe n’ibintu bidashobora gusobanura
urebye ikosa ryakozwe.
– Ibimenyetso bidafashije byerekana ukekwa cyangwa abakekwa ; umusomyi
n’ingenza baba bafite amahirwe angana yo gusubiza ikibazo.
– Akenshi muri izi nkuru ukekwa arafatwa ariko icyaha yafungiwe kikongera
kikaba, bigahita bigaragara ko atari we bigasa n’ibisubiye irudubi.
– Kwitegereza neza ibintu no kubyibazaho.

234

–
–
–

Igisubizo kijyanye n’ibyabaye gikomeza kuba urujijo.
Kwigizayo ibidashoboka byose mu gushakisha igisubizo. Umunyacyaha
avumburwa uhereye ku byo wagiye ubona, ntibapfa kumugwaho
by’agatunguro cyangwa ngo yivemo.
Igisigara nubwo cyaba kitakekwaga ni cyo kiba ari igisubizo nyacyo.

Umwitozo

Nyuma yo kwiga amoko y’inkuru ndende, umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gutanga
ingero zinyuranye ziturutse mu zindi ndimi bitewe n’amashami biga. Abasaba gutanga
ibisubanuro bigaragaza impamvu izo ngero zashyizwe muri ayo moko, umwarimu
akabafasha kubinonosora..

e) Uko basesengura inkuru ndende

Iyo basesengura inkuru ndende bibanda kuri ibi bikurikira: ikivugwa mu nkuru,
abanyarubuga, ibarankuru, akabugankuru, ibikorwa, umugendo w’inkuru,
uburebure n’ahantu. Ibi bigakorwa hakurikijwe amahange n’amahame y’ingenzi
akoreshwa mu gusesengura umwandiko w’ubuvanganzo muri rusange.

Ikivugwa mu nkuru ndende

Ingingo abanditsi b’inkuru ndende bavugaho ni nyinshi kandi ziratandukanye
kimwe n’izo dusanga mu zindi ngeri z’ubuvanganzo. Mu nkuru ndende dusangamo
urukundo rudasibangana, urukundo rwa bugigi, uburere n’umuco wa kera bitajya
imbizi n’uburere n’umuco by’ubu, poritiki n’ubutegetsi, ubwenge bw’indushyi, uburaya
n’ubwomanzi, iyimukacyaro, ubuhemu, ubugome, ishyari, inzangano, amoko, urupfu
rutunamura icumu, ubusabane mu bantu, ubukene n’ubujiji, inkuru ndende zivuga
ku bukoroni n’ibindi.

Abakinankuru (abanyarubuga)

Mu nkuru ndende haba umukinankuru mukuru ushobora kuba umwe cyangwa
babiri. Umukinankuru mukuru ni we uba ari ipfundo ry’inkuru. Ni we ikigamijwe
cyangwa intego y’inkuru iba ishingiyeho. Uyu ni we insanganyamatsiko rusange
ivugwa mu nkuru iba ishingiyeho.
Hari kandi n’abakinankuru bungirije. Aba ni bo usanga mu nkuru bamufasha
kugera ku kigamijwe cyangwa bakamubera imbogamizi. Aba bakinankuru kandi ni
na bo usanga insanganyamatsiko nto cyangwa zungirije zishingiyeho.
Mu nkuru ndende kandi dusangamo cyangwa dushobora gusangamo abakinankuru
ntagombwa, aba bakinankuru iyo urebye usanga kuba mu nkuru kwabo cyangwa
kutagaragaramo nta cyo byahindura ku kivugwa mu nkuru. Nta nsanganyamatsiko
iba ibashingiyeho. Mu yandi magambo twabita indorerezi.

235

Ibarankuru

Mu nkuru ndende, ibarankuru ni kimwe mu biranga inkuru ndende. Hanagaragazwa
ko ibarankuru rishobora gukorwa ku buryo bune:
–
–
–
–

Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru na we ubwe akinamo. Ni muri urwo
rwego usanga akoresha ngenga ya kabiri.
Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru ari hanze yayo. Aha usanga akoresha
ngenga ya gatatu asa n’uvuga ibintu yareberaga iruhande mu gihe byabaga.
Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru ye ubwe. Ni muri urwo rwego usanga
akoresha ngenga ya mbere kuko ibyo avuga aba abivuga kuri we.
Umubarankuru ashobora kandi kubara inkuru yiha gutekerereza umukinankuru.
Mu kubara inkuru kwe usanga yiha kubara ibyo umukinankuru yatekerezaga igihe
amubaraho inkuru.

Ikitonderwa:
Umubarankuru atandukanye n’umwanditsi w’inkuru. Umwanditsi w’inkuru ni
umuhanzi wanditse inkuru ibarwa mu gitabo ke. Muri uko kwandika inkuru ye agena
uburyo ibarwa. Muri ubwo buryo ibarwamo haba hari umuntu ugenda uyibara, uwo
akaba ari we mubarankuru. Cyakora hari igihe umwanditsi ashobora kuba ari na we
mubarankuru igihe abara inkuru y’ubuzima bwe.

Akabuga nkuru (ahantu)

Inkuru ndende kandi irangwa no kuba ifite aho ibarirwa; ni ukuvuga akabuga nkuru.
Mu nkuru ndende akabuga nkuru gashobora kuba kazwi cyangwa ari agahimbano.
Iyo uwandika inkuru avuga ibyabaye ashobora no kuvuga mu by’ukuri aho byabereye
hazwi. Iyo abara inkuru y’ibitarabayeho, cyakora bishobora kubaho mu buzima
rusange, ashobora gukoresha akabuga nkuru gahimbano; akavuga ibintu byabereye
ahantu runaka ariko hatazwi ku ikarita y’isi.

Ibikorwa

Ibikorwa mu nkuru ndende bishingira ku bakinankuru cyane cyane ku mukinankuru
mukuru. We n’abakinankuru bungirije bashinzwe kuyobora imigendekere y’ibikorwa
byo mu nkuru kugeza ku ndunduro y’inkuru. Bamwe barema imbaraga zimufasha
kugera ku ntego umwanditsi w’inkuru aba yamuhaye. Nk’uko abasesenguzi b’inkuru
ndende babyemeza, inkuru ndende iyo ari yo yose irangwa no kuba ishobora
gukorerwa ishushanyabikorwa.
Ishushanyabikorwa / inyabune nshushanyangingo mu nkuru ndende
Abakinnyi b’imena bafatanyije n’abungirije bayobora imigendekere y’ibikorwa mu
nkuru ndende kugeza ku ndunduro y’inkuru. Bityo mu nkuru ndende umusesenguzi
ashobora gushushanya ibikorwa yifashishije igishushanyo giteye gitya:

236

Ugenera/
Nyakugaba/
Umugenga

Ikigamijwe/
Intego
Igiharanirwa

Ugenerwa/nyiri
inyungu

Abafasha/
Inyunganizi

Nyiri ubwite/
Ruhamwa/
Icyatwa

Imbogamizi.

Nyiri ubwite: uyu ni we mukinankuru mukuru inkuru iba ishingiyeho, ni we uba
ufite intego agamije kugeraho muri iyo nkuru. Aba ashobora kuyigeraho cyangwa
ntayigereho.
Ikigamijwe: ni icyo umukinankuru mukuru aba agamije kugeraho mu nkuru. Ni
intego aba yahawe n’umwanditsi w’inkuru.
Ugenera: ni igituma umukinankuru mukuru agira intego afite muri iyo nkuru.
Ashobora kuba undi mukinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora gutuma agira
intego runaka.
Ugenerwa: mu yandi magambo ni nyiri inyungu; ni uwo ari we wese mu nkuru wagira
icyo yunguka mu gihe umukinankuru mukuru ageze ku cyo yari agamije mu nkuru.
Abafasha: ni abakinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora, mu nkuru, gutuma
umukinankuru mukuru agera ku cyo yari agamije, cyangwa ikigerageza kumushyigikira
mu rugendo rwe rwose kimufasha, kabone n’iyo atakigeraho mu irangira ry’inkuru.
Imbogamizi: ni abakinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora, mu nkuru,
gutuma umukinankuru mukuru atagera ku cyo yari agamije, cyangwa ikigerageza
kumubangamira mu rugendo rwe rwose kimubuza amahirwe kabone n’iyo yagera ku
cyo yari agamije mu irangira ry’inkuru, ariko kikaba cyamubangamiraga.

Umugendo w’inkuru

Mu nkuru, ibikorwa bishobora gukurikirana uko byagiye biba mu mateka yabyo.
Cyakora abanditsi b’inkuru ndende b’abahanga, usanga bakora ibyo twakwita
gukwikira inkuru, bakagenda babara inkuru nyinshi icyarimwe kandi zidakurikiranye
uko zabaye mu mateka. Umwe mu basesenguzi b’inkuru ndende T. Todorov mu gitabo
ke “Théorie de la littérature, 2001” asobanura ko hari uburyo butatu bwo gukwikira
inkuru ari bwo kuyikurikiranya mu mateka yayo (a, b, c, d,..), kuyikiranya (a, b, a, b,
c, d, c, d,..) no kuyivangavanga (z, a, c, e,...).

Uburebure

Inkuru ndende nk’uko izina ryayo ribigaragaza, irangwa no kuba ari ndende koko.
Kuba hari uburyo ibikorwa bikurikirana kandi bigenda bitera amatsiko usoma

237

ku buryo atarambirwa n’uburebure bwayo. Uburebure bw’inkuru ndende kandi
bugaragarira mu inyuranamo ry’inkuru nyinshi zitadukanye kandi ritarambirana.

Inyigisho irimo n’indangagaciro

Buri nkuru cyangwa buri gihangano cy’ubuvanganzo kiba gikubiyemo inyigisho
n’indangagaciro runaka, ni ngombwa ko usesengura inkuru abigaragaza.

Inshamake

Usesengura inkuru kandi aba agomba gukora inshamake yayo, igaragaramo iby’ingenzi
bivugwamo.

Umwanditsi w’inkuru

Ni ngombwa ko usesengura agaragaza ubuzima bw’umwanditsi w’inkuru ndetse
n’ibindi bihangano bye. Ibi bishobora kumufasha guhuza ibivugwa mu nkuru
n’ubuzima busanzwe.

f) Itandukaniro ry’inkuru ndende n’inkuru ngufi by’umwihariko
Inkuru ndende

- Inkuru ndende igira abakinankuru
benshi n’ibikorwa byinshi
- Inkuru ndende isobekeranya inkuru
nyinshi nubwo insanganyamatsiko aba
ari imwe.
- Umugendo w’inkuru ndende usanga
ugenda ucurikiranya ibikorwa.
- Usanga utubuga nkuru cyangwa se
ahakinirwa ari henshi.
- Inkuru ndende yo ishobora no kuvuga
ibya kera.

Inkuru ngufi

- Inkuru ngufi igira abakinankuru bake
n’ibikorwa bike.
- Inkuru ngufi iba yubakiye ku nkuru
imwe rukumbi.
- Umugendo w’inkuru ngufi uba ugiye
umujyo umwe.
- Aho inkuru ibera ntihagomba kuba
henshi.
- Inkuru ngufi iba ivuga ibintu bishya,
byabaye bidasanzwe.

Umukoro

Nyuma yo kumurika ibyavuye mu matsinda umwarimu aha abanyeshuri umukoro
wo guhanga inkuru ndede ku nsanganyamatsiko iri mu gitabo cy’umunyeshuri. Iyo
barangije, umwarimu ahitamo imwe mu nkuru zahimbwe abona ko yubahirije uturango
tw’inkuru ndende n’indi abona ko itubahirije uturango twayo akazisesengurira
abanyeshuri bagerageza kuzijora.
Umukoro wo guhanga inkuru ndende wandika
Ugendeye ku mabwiriza agenga imyandikire n’imiterere y’inkuru ndende, tangira
uhimbe wandika agace k’inkuru ndende ku nsanganyamatsiko wihitiyemo
n’ubwoko bw’inkuru wihitiyemo ku buryo uzayisomera bagenzi bawe mu ruhame.
(Ntimurenze impapuro 20 kandi ntimuge hasi impapuro 10.)

238

Intera ya gatanu: Ururimi n’ubwumvane
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku kaganiro yasomye no ku bushakashatsi yakoze, nyuma y’iyi ntera
umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Kurondora no gusobanura amafatizo y’ubwumvane.
– Gusobanura imimaro y’ururimi no kuyisobanura.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’igitabo
k’iyigandimi.

Imitangire y’isomo

1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kongera gusoma inkuru ya Rugigana n’ingoma ye
maze akabasaba kugaragaza uvuga, ubwirwa n’ikivugwa.
2. Isesengura:
Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gukorera ubushakashatsi mu matsinda bagerageza
gusubiza ibyo bibazo byabajijwe ku kaganiro no gukora ubundi bushakashatsi
bwimbitse ku rurimi n’ubwumvane. Abaha igihe cyo kubikora cyagera akabasaba
kuzaza kumurika ibyavuye mu matsinda akabafasha kubinonosora bigakorerwa
inshamake ikandikwa ku kibaho no mu makayi yabo.
Urugero rw’ibyava mu bushakashatsi:

Inshoza y’ubwumvane

Ururimi urwo ari rwo rwose, rutuma abaruvuga iyo bava bakagera bumvana, umwe
yavuga, undi agasobanukirwa n’ibyo avuze. Ibyo bituma umuntu abasha kugeza
ku bandi ibyo atekereza, ibyo yaba azi bo batazi cyangwa se akabashushanyiriza
amagambo, ibyo yabonye ariko bo batabona n’amaso yabo muri ako kanya bavugana.
Ibi byerekana ko ururimi ari igikoresho cy’ubwumvane. Kubera iyo mpamvu, ururimi
ni inzira y’ingenzi n’ikigega k’ibitekerezo n’imico y’abo ruhuza. Ururimi n’ubwumvane
bishobora kwigwa mu mpushya enye z’ingenzi : amafatizo y’ubwumvane, indanguruzi
y’ubwumvane, imimaro y’ururimi n’amategeko ngombwa agenga ubwumvane.

Amafatizo y’ubwumvane.

1. Uvuga / Nyakuvuga: ni we utangiza ikiganiro, agakomoza ku kivugwaho. Aba afite
uwo abwira agakenera kubona igisubizo mu magambo cyangwa se mu bikorwa
ku byo yavuze.
2. Ubwirwa / Nyakubwirwa: Yakira ibyo agejejweho n’uvuga. Ubwirwa agomba
kumva no gusobanukirwa neza n’ibyo yabwiwe mbere y’uko atanga igisubizo.

239

3. Ingambo/ubutumwa: ni ubutumwa nyirizina uvuga ageza ku ubwirwa.
4. Inzira/umuyoboro: Duhereye ku buryo ibivugwa bigenda bikagera ku ubwirwa,
habaho inzira mbona nkubone cyangwa inzira mbonera iyo uvuga avugana n’uwo
abwira amaso ku yandi. Iyo uvuga yumvikanye n’uwo abwira haciyemo akanya,
inzira ikivugwa cyaciyemo iba ibaye inzira iziguye. Habaho n’inzira itaziguye nk’iyo
hakoreshejwe terefone cyangwa ibindi bikoresho by’itumanaho bikora nka yo.
5. Inkurikizo cyangwa interamvugo: ururimi cyangwa imvugo ya nyakuvuga
byumvikanisha mu buryo bufatika inshoza y’ahantu cyangwa y’igihe y’ikivugwaho.
Interamvugo rero cyangwa inkurikizo ni ururimi, ibimenyetso cyangwa amarenga.
Uvuga ashobora gukoresha imvugo cyangwa inyandiko. Muri iyi mvugo cyangwa
inyandiko byumvwa cyangwa bigasomwa nyakuvuga aba ashaka kugeza ku
ubwirwa ubutumwa. Ibi twabigereranya n’ibyapa byo ku muhanda biyobora gusa
ababiziranyeho. Ni bo baba basobanukiwe neza n’ubutumwa bitanga cyangwa
n’ikivugwa na byo.
6. Ikivugwaho: ni icyo abavugana baba bavuganaho. Abavugana bagomba kuba
bagihuriyeho, bakiziranyeho.

Indanguruzi y’ubwumvane

Dore uko ubwumvane bugenda bukurikije indanguruzi yabwo.

Ikivugwaho

Uvuga/
Nyakuvuga

Ubutumwa/
Ingambo
Inzira/
umuyoboro

Ubwirwa
Nyakubwirwa

Inkurikizo/
Interamvuga
Imimaro y’ururimi/nyarurimi
Ururimi ni igikoresho k’ingenzi mu buzima bw’umuntu. Abantu bose iyo bava
bakagera iyo bashyikirana mu biganiro, mu mbwirwaruhame, mu ikinamico, mu
misango y’ubukwe... bakoresha ururimi. Uwagize ikibazo akagira ubumuga bwo
kutavuga, bwo kudasohora ijwi, agira uburyo yihariye acishamo ubutumwa bwe
bukagera ku bo ashaka ko bamwumva nta shiti.

240

Abahanga mu iyigandimi, basanze ururimi rugira imimaro myinshi, ariko iy’ingenzi
ni umumaro nyakuvuga, umumaro nyakubwirwa, umumaro nkurikizo, umumaro
nyanzira, umumaro nyangambo.

1) Umumaro nyakuvuga/nsesekaza

Uvuga yifashisha ururimi cyangwa imvugo kugira ngo avuge, yumvikanishe mu
buryo butaziguye akamuri ku mutima. Bamwe bawita kandi umumaro nsesekaza
cyangwa nsesekazamutima, wibanda ku gukoresha nganga ya mbere.
Ingero:
1. Umugeni ageze mu mpinga ati: Ahwi! Nari mfuye Mana y’i Rwanda!
2. Mbega we! Ntuzi ibyabaye!
3. Icyampa nkazatabaruka nirereye abana.

Ntitwabura kuvuga ko hari n’abanditsi bakoresha uyu mumaro bashyira ikivugwa ku
musomyi kandi mu by’ukuri atari umusomyi kivuga.

2) Umumaro nyakubwirwa

Izingiro ry’uyu mumaro ni nyakubwirwa. Wumvisha ubwirwa ko ari we shingiro,
ko ari we urebwa n’ubwumvane. Hakoreshwa ngenga ya kabiri hagamijwe:
– Gusobanura ubwirwa mu bandi.
– Kumukesha umutima ngo yakire ibyo amubwiye.
– Kumuhwitura…
Uyu mumaro ugaragarira cyanecyane mu nteko no mu mvugo yumvikanisha
guhamagara. Soma ingero zikurikira zibyerekana.
Ingero:
Igihe utanga ikiganiro kuri SIDA, ugasobanura uko yamenyekanye, uko yandura, uko
ivurwa, ubushakashatsi ku miti n’inkingo… Noneho ukarangiza wereka abo ubwira
ko ruriye abandi rutabibagiwe ko na bo ishobora kubageraho baramutse batirinze.

3) Umumaro nkurikizo

Ni ibimenyetso byose byerekana icyo ubutumwa bwerekejeho. Amagambo
akoreshwa aha uyabwirwa n’ishusho y’ahantu cyangwa y’ibihe ibivugwa
bihererejweho. Bamwe bawita kandi umumaro ndengarurimi cyangwa
nsobanuzi.

Urugero:
– Ni ukuvuga ko ejo nzaba mpari.
– Bishatse gusobanura ko ibintu bikomeye.

241

4) Umumaro nyabusizi (nyaburanga/nyangambo)

Mu bwumvane, inzira yose igira uburyo bwayo. Nyakuvuga yifashisha
ururimi, bityo agakoresha imvugo cyangwa inyandiko. Imiryohere y’inganzo
yose uzayisanga muri uyu mumaro. Injyana, isubirajwi, isubirajambo, amoko
y’imizimizo cyangwa inyandiko inogeye amaso ndetse n’amagambo y’ikivugwa
uko yakabaye hatitaweho ibisobanuro byayo, ibyo byose bigaragaza umumaro
nyangambo w’ururimi bita kandi nyabusizi.
Ingero:

1. Udutoki dutatu dutonze udutemeri.
2. Isha y’umushishe y’ishashi ishoka icitse ijosi.

3. Ugiye bagushishira uzagaruka bagushishimuye.
4.

« Nsanze ari ijuru ry’umwezi
Nange mpimbiraho umwato
Ni ko kumwita umwanga kurutwa
Nsubiye mwita Marebe yera
Kandi atembaho amaribori
Ni umutako w’urutanisha
Ni ubutijima bw’urukundo
Ni urukenyerero rw’inkindi
Nkunda inkesha ze z’umukwira. »
(Rugamba C., Amibukiro, pp.34).

5) Umumaro nyanzira

Uyu mumaro tuwusanga mu magambo ya nyakuvuga mu gihe atangiye, akomeje
cyangwa se asubitse ubwumvane cyangwa se agenzura ko umuyoboro (inzira)
ari ntamakemwa. Iyi nzira y’ubwumvane ishobora kuba umurongo wa terefoni,
ishusho, igishushanyo cyangwa se n’ibindi bikoresho by’itumanaho byakora nka
terefoni. Aha rero ururimi rugira umumaro wo gutangira, gukomeza, gusigasira,
gusubika, gusubukura cyangwa se guhagarika ubwumvane.

Ingero:
Aro!
Birumvikana?
Si byo se?
Urebye rero…
Nyine…
Nawe urabyumva…
Nuko…
Mbese mwaramutse bantu bakuru?

242

6) Umumaro nyakivugwa

Iyo umuntu avuga hari ubutumwa cyangwa amakuru aba ashaka gutanga, akaba
ari amakuru afitiye gihamya. Abavugana bagombye kuba baziranyeho. Amagambo
akoreshwa aha abwirwa ishusho y’ahantu cyangwa y’ibihe ibivuga behererejweho
n’imiterere yabyo.

Urugero:
Ni ya nzu y’igorofa y’amazu abiri twabonye ejo iri mu ruhande rw’iburyo
bw’umuhanda wa kaburimbo
Dore uko imimaro y’ubwumvane igaragara ku ndanguruzi y’ubwumvane:

Ikitonderwa:

Nta nyandiko cyangwa umwandiko wiharirwa n’umumaro w’ururimi umwe
gusa. Imimaro myinshi ishobora kugaragarira icyarimwe mu mwandiko cyangwa
mu kivugwa kimwe mu buryo no mu bwiganze butandukanye. Cyakora kugira
ngo itumanaho ribe ryuzuye imimaro yose igomba kuba irimo akaba ari na byo
bitandukanya imvugo y’umuntu n’iy’inyamaswa.

Amategeko ngombwa agenga ubwumvane

Kugira ngo ubwumvane bushoboke, hari amategeko ngombwa agomba kubahirizwa.
Uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba:
– Guhuza inkurikizo: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba kuba bahuje ururimi cyangwa
ibimenyetso bakoresha.

243

–
–

Guhurira ku kivugwa: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba kuba bafite ikivugwa
baziranyeho. Iyo bataziranye ku kivugwa ntibashobora kumvikana.
Guhuza inzira: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba guhuza uburyo bakoresha kugira ngo
bumvikane. Niba ari terefone bombi bagomba kuba bayifite, yaba ari ibaruwa,
bombi bakaba bazi gusoma.

Umwitozo

Amatsinda yose arangije kumurika ibyo yagezeho no kubikorera ubugororangingo,
umwarimu atanga imyitozo kugira ngo agenzure ko intego z’isomo zagezweho.
Iyo myitozo ikorwa na buri mu nyeshuri ku gitike igakosorerwa hamwe mu ishuri
umwarimu akabafasha kunonosora ibisubizo bikwiye.

Umwitozo n’ibisubizo :

1. Mu Rwanda iyo Abanyarwanda bashyikirana bakoresha inzira zitandukanye.
Andika inzira zose ubona zishoboka zaba zikoreshwa n’uvuga kugira ngo
ikivugwa kigere ku ubwirwa. Izira zishobora gukoreshwa n’uvuga mu Rwanda
kugira ngo ikivugwa kigere ku ubwirwa ni izi zikurikira : inzira mbona nkubone,
ibaruwa, terefoni, insakazamajwi, insakazamashusho, imbuga nkoranyambaga
n’ibindi.
2. Wifashishije indanguruzi y’ubwumvane, garagaza amasano agenga
ubwumvane mu misango y’ubukwe. Amasano y’amafatizo y’ubwumvane mu
misango y’ubukwe ku ndanguruzi y’ubwumvane ateye ku buryo bukurikira:

Igisubizo gishoboka:
Umuganga uha ikiganiro abaturage

Muri iki kiganiro nifuza ko twaganira ku ndwara ya SIDA (umumaro nyakivugwa
1 )Iryo jambo ni impine y’Igifaransa, umuntu agenekereje yavuga ko bisobanura
«Ubwandu buterwa n’igabanuka ry’abasirikare barinda umubiri bagatuma utakaza
ubudahangarwa (Umumaro ndengarurimi/ nsobanuzi 2) Yavumbuwe mu myaka
ya za 1980, ikaba iterwa n’agakoko ko mu bwoko bwa virusi. Ikindi kizwi ni uko
ikunze kwibasira abantu bakora imibonano mpuzabitsina nubwo n’ibindi byiciro
by’abantu itabirebera izuba. Kugeza ubu hari imiti ishobora kugabanya ubukana
bwayo ariko nta rukingo ruraboneka nubwo hari izagiye zigeragezwa (umumaro
nyakivugwa 3) Muri aka kanya nkaba nifuza kubakangurira kuyirinda kuko yica
kandi ikaba imaze kuba icyorezo cyugarije isi yose (Umumaro nyakubwirwa 4).
Muranyumva? (Umumaro nzira /nyakuzo 5). Irica. Nyamara hari uburyo bwinshi
bwo kuyirinda. (Umumaro nyakivugwa 6) agapfa kaburiwe ni impongo kandi ngo
urwishigishiye ararusoma. (Umumaro nyakubwirwa 7 + umumaro nyabusizi 8)
Mushatse mwahindura imyitwarire. Cyanecyane urubyiruko kuko ari rwo rwugarijwe.
(Umumaro nyakubwirwa 9) Mbabazwa gusa n’uko bamwe muri mwe mwigize ba
ntibindeba. (Umumaro nyakuvuga cyangwa nsesekazamutima 10).
Ibisobanuro:
– (1, 3 na 6): Muganga aratanga amakuru ku kivugwaho (SIDA).
– (2): Aragerageza gusobanura impine SIDA kuko asanze itumvikana ku bantu
batari inzobere mu byerekeye indwara.
– (4, 7 na 9): Aragerageza kumvisha abo abwira ko ibyo avuga bibareba kandi ko
bagomba kubyubahiriza.
– (5): Arabaza ikibazo ariko si ukuvuga ko akeneye igisubizo; ahubwo arashaka
kumenya niba bamukurikira anatsindagira ibyo amaze kuvuga.
– (8): Aragerageza kuryoshya imvugo akoresheje imigenurano.
– (10): Aragaragaza icyo atekereza ku byo amaze kubabwira yerekana ko bimubabaje
(imbamutima).

245

Inshamake y’ibikubiye muri uyu mutwe wa munani
Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo binyuranye bituma bashobora kuvuga
iby’ingenzi bikubiye mu mutwe wa munani. Ibyo bibazo yababaza bishobora
gutuma batanga inshamake ikurikira:
– Inkuru ndende ari ingeri ubuvanganzo nyandiko irangwa no kuba ari ndende,
kugira abakinankuru benshi, gusobekeranya ingingo nyinshi no kubara inkuru
y’ibyabayeho cyangwa ibitarabayeho ariko bishushanya ubuzima rusange
tubamo. Inkuru ndende itandukanye n’inkuru ngufi ku burebure bwayo, ku kuba
ifite abakinankuru benshi, ku kuba igira insanganyamatsiko nyinshi. Inkuru
ndende itandukanye n’umugani kuko mu mugani havugwamo ibitarabayeho
kandi bidashobora no kubaho mu buzima rusange.
– Ururimi ni igikoresho gituma abavugana aho bava bakagera bashobora kuganira
bakumvikana. Ubwo bwumvane bushingiye ku bintu bitandatu by’ingenzi ari
byo : uvuga, ubwirwa, ingambo, inzira, interamvugo/ inkurikizo, ikivugwaho.
Amasano y’ayo mafatizo ashobora kwerekanishwa igishushanyo kitwa
indanguruzi y’ubwumvane.
– Umumaro nyanteramvugo cyangwa nkurikizo ushingiye ku gusubanur ubutumwa
butangwa. Ibyo bishobora guhita bisobanuka mu magambo yakoreshejwe.
Bishobora kugaragara mu myubakire y’interuro ariko inkurikizo yo mu magambo
ntibe mu bisobanuro asanganywe. Ibyo tubisanga aho amagambo asobanurwa
n’ayo biri kumwe. Umumaro nyabusizi uba mu miryohereze y’injyana n’isubirajwi
n’isubirajambo binogeye amatwi, bikamera nk’umutako w’ikivugwa nyirizina.
– Kugira ngo habeho ubwumvane, uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba guhuza neza
inkurikizo, guhurira ku kivugwa no guhuza inzira y’ubwumvane.

Ibibazo n’ibisubizo by’isuzuma rusange risoza umutwe wa munani
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)

Ibigenderwaho mu isuzuma:

– Ubushobozi bwo gusesengura inkuru ndende.
– Ubushobozi bwo guhanga umwandiko w’inkuru ndende.
– Ubushobozi bwo gusobanura amafatizo y’ubwumvane, gusobanura imimaro
y’ubwumvane no gutahura mu mwandiko yasomye imimaro y’ubwumvane.

Uyu mwitozo ukorwa n’umunyeshuri ku giti ke nyuma yawo umwarimu aramukosora
akareba ibibazo byihariye buri munyeshuri afite kugira ngo arebe uko yamufasha
mbere yo gutangira undi mutwe.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva no gusesengura umwandiko

1. Ingingo nkuru n’izungirije zavuzweho cyane mu mwandiko ni izihe?
		– Ingaruka z’ubukoroni
		– Akarengane k’Abanyafurika
		– Ikandamizwa ry’Abanyarwanda
		 – Imirimo y’agahato ku bw’abakoroni
		 – Amashuri mu gihe cy’ubukoroni
		 – Ibihingwa bishya igihe cy’ubukoroni…
2. Kuba tugikenera inkunga y’amahanga ntitwabifata nk’ubukoroni?
Sobanura. Byafatwa nk’ubukoroni bushya kuko nk’iyo habonetse inkunga
y’amahanga, bene kuyitanga bohereza rimwe na rimwe abazakurikirana

248

3.

4.

5.

imikoreshereze yayo. Iyo bitabaye ibyo, usanga hari izindi nyungu za
politiki ziba zihishe inyuma y’izo nkunga. Hari imyanzuro ishobora
gufatirwa igihugu runaka cyo muri Afurika ngo kuko yenda kitakoze ibyo
Abanyaburayi n’ahandi bakeneye.
Abanyafurika mu mpande zitandukanye bigaragaje bate berekana
akarengane kabo? Bakoze iyo bwabaga, barwana inkundura, bamwe
barwanyije Abongereza, abandi barwanya Abadage n’Ababirigi, Abafaransa
n’abandi kugeza igihe ibihugu bimwe bitangiye kugera ku bwigenge nyuma
yo kumena amaraso.
Hari ingingo zihamya ko Abanyarwanda bahoze bumvikana,
bafashanya. Zirondore. Abanyarwanda bari batuje, bari bapfunditse
ubumwe, basabanaga amazi, barahuranaga umuriro, baranywanaga
ntihagire uhirahira ngo atatire igihango, bafatanyaga kwivuna umwanzi,
baratabaranaga n’ibindi.
Poritiki n’inyigisho n’imikorere y’abakoroni byari byiza byose.
Sobanura utarondogoye. Ntibyari byiza byose. Abakoroni barasahuye,
batsikamiye abaturage, bahinduye imyemerere yabo bazana Imana y’i
Burayi, bikubiye ubutaka bwera n’ibindi. Ariko Abakoroni ku rundi ruhande
bazanye amashuri abantu biga gusoma no kwandika, imihanda iraharurwa,
ibihingwa bishya bigera ku butaka bw’Afurika ndetse n’amabuye y’agaciro
aracukurwa n’ubwo amenshi yajyanwaga i Burayi.
Poritiki Abakoroni bakoresheje kugira ngo batwigarurire ni iyihe?
Yatugizeho izihe ngaruka? Bakoresheje poritiki yo gucamo ibice abaturage.
Abantu bamaze kumva ko atari bamwe batangiye kuryana no guhembera
urwikekwe. Nko mu Rwanda amaherezo amacakubiri n’urwikekwe byaje
kuvamo ivanguramoko ryashyizwe mu bikorwa haba Jenoside Yakorewe
Abatutsi muri Mata 1994. Muri Afurika handi haracyagaragara ibikingi
by’abazungu.
Ijwi ry’abaturage ni ijwi ry’Imana. Iyi mvugo urayemera? Sobanura.
Nk’uko benshi babyemeza nange sinabihakana kandi ni ko bimeze. Iyo
abantu bagukunze, iyo wumvikana n’abantu urakundwa ugakundwakazwa,
bishoboka bite rero ko Imana itaha umugisha imibanire yawe na bo?
Icyo rubanda rwishyizemo, rwakunze n’Imana iba yabihaye umugisha,
ibishyigikiye.

II. Inyunguramagambo

a) Shaka ibisobanuro by’aya magambo akurikira ukurikije uko
yakoreshejwe mu mwandiko umaze gusoma.
		1. Kazungu: umukoroni, gashakabuhake, umunyaburayi wakoronije
Abanyafurika.
		2. Kurahurana umuriro: kumvikana, kuba hafi y’umuvandimwe ku buryo
atewe wamuvuna.

249

		3. Intandaro: urwenzo rwo kuba ikintu runaka cyaba.
		4. Ak’imuhana: ibintu byose by’imahanga, byo mu mihana udafiteho
uburenganzira.
		5. Ibisigisigi: ibimenyetso bigaragaza inkurikizi y’amateka, y’ibintu byabaye
mu gihe runaka.
		6. Gutwaza kiboko: kuyoborana igitugu, imbaraga n’agahato gakandamiza.
		7. Gucuza: kwambura, kwaka, kunyaga abantu, ibintu uburenganzira
n’ibintu bari bafite.
		8. Umuco: imigenzereze n’imimerere y’abantu harimo indangagaciro na
kirazira by’abatuye ibunaka.
		9. Guceba: kugabanya agaciro k’ikintu, kugaya, guhinyura akamaro k’ikintu.

n’ibisobanuro byayo byatanzwe mu ruhushya B ukoresheje akambi aho
bishoboka.

III.Iyigandimi

a) Sobanura amafatizo y’ubwumvane.

		1. Uvuga : ni we utangiza ikiganiro, agatangiza n’ikivugwaho. Aba afite
uwo abwira agakenera kubona igisubizo mu magambo cyangwa se mu
bikorwa ku byo yavuze.
		2. Ubwirwa : Yakira ibyo agejejweho n’uvuga. Ubwirwa agomba kumva
no gusobanukirwa neza n’ibyo yabwiwe mbere y’uko atanga igisubizo.
		3. Ingambo : ni ubutumwa nyiri izina uvuga ageza ku ubwirwa.
		4. Inzira : Duhereye ku buryo ibivugwa bigenda bikagera ku ubwirwa,
habaho inzira mbona nkubone cyangwa inzira mbonera iyo uvuga
ahanganye n’uwo abwira amaso ku maso. Iyo uvuga yumvikanye n’uwo
abwira haciyemo akanya, inzira ikivugwa cyaciyemo iba ibaye inzira
iziguye. Habaho n’inzira itaziguye nk’iyo hakoreshejwe telefone cyangwa
ibindi bikoresho by’itumanaho bikora nka yo.
		5. Inkurikizo cyangwa interamvugo : ururimi cyangwa imvugo ya
nyakuvuga byumvikanisha mu buryo bufatika inshoza y’ahantu cyangwa
y’igihe y’ikivugwaho. Interamvugo rero cyangwa inkurikizo ni ururimi,
ibimenyetso cyangwa amarenga. Uvuga ashobora gukoresha imvugo
cyangwa inyandiko, cyangwa bigasomwa nyakuvuga ashaka kugeza
ku ubwirwa ikivugwa/ubutumwa. Ibi twabigereranya n’ibyapa byo ku
muhanda biyobora gusa ababiziranyeho. Ni bo baba basobanukiwe neza
n’ubutumwa bitanga cyangwa n’ikivugwa na byo.
		6. Ikivugwaho : ni icyo abavugana baba bavuganaho. Abavugana bagomba
kuba bagihuriyeho, bakiziranyeho.
b) Uhereye ku mwandiko “Kwihesha agaciro”, tahura imimaro y’ururimi
usanga mu nteruro zikurikira zavuye mu mwandiko wasomye.
		1. Kera ba kazungu bataragera i Rwanda. (Kera: umumaro nyakivugwa
yumvisha igihe), (i Rwanda: nyakivugwa yumvisha ahantu.)
		2. Amatwara yabo atwambura ireme, agaciro kacu karacuya. (Umumaro
nyakuvuga + nyangambo)
		3. Tuzi ibipfa n’ibikira, tuzi kwihitiramo. (umumaro nyakuvuga ( tuzi)
		4. Twirinde gutwarwa kiboko na kazungu. (umumaro nyakuvuga na
nyakubwirwa.)
		5. Twese ntitwatokobwe byinshi! (umumaro nyakuvuga na nyakubwirwa.)
		6. Mpana uwange yananira nti: “Ramvura ingoma wime!” (umumaro
nyakuvuga na nyakubwirwa.)

251

		7. None se Kiriziya Gaturika yari izwi na ba nde? (umumaro nyakuvuga
na nyakubwirwa.)
		8. Kugeza ubu se tuba tucyambara impuzu, ishabure n’inkanda? (umumaro
nyakuvuga na nyakubwirwa)
		9. Reka tuvuge ko hari ibyiza ubukoroni bwadusigiye. (umumaro
nyakuvuga.)
		10. Oya, sigaho! Nitwikosore duce ukubiri no guhora duteze “barampa”.
(umumaro nyakubwirwa)

III.Guhanga

Vuga uturango tw’inkuru ndende ugomba kumenya kugira ngo uhange
inkuru ndende yubahirije amategeko.
– Inkuru ndende irangwa n’imiterere yayo; ibarankuru, abakinankuru (abantu
cyangwa inyamaswa), kuba hari ikivugwa, kuba hari ahantu n’igihe kibera,
kuba ari ndende (akenshi hagati y’impapuro 100 na 250) kuba hari uburyo
ibikorwa bikurikirana kandi bigenda bitera amatsiko usoma, ku buryo
atarambirwa gusoma inkuru ibarwa.

Imyitozo nzamurabushobozi
–
–

Umwarimu atahura ikigero cy’ubushobozi bwa buri munyeshuri ahereye ku
manota bagize mu isuzuma risoza umutwe akabashyira mu matsinda akurikije
ingorane bafite.
Umwarimu abaha imyitozo ijyanye n’ibyiciro byabo bakayikorera hamwe mu
matsinda. Ku badafite ibibazo, umwarimu abavanga muri ya matsinda y’abafite
ibibazo kugira ngo bafashe abandi gusobanukirwa neza n’imyitozo bakora
cyangwa se akabasaba gukora imyitozo nyagurabushobozi. Iyi myitozo umwarimu
ayitegura ashingiye ku bibazo abanyeshuri be bafite yibanda aho abanyeshuri be
bagaragaje ubushobozi buke.

Urugero rw’imyitozo yabaha:
1. Andika inzira zishobora gukoreshwa n’uvuga mu kigo wigaho kugira ngo
ikivugwa ke kigere k’ubwirwa. Inzira zishobora gukoreshwa n’uvuga mu kigo
kugira ngo ikivugwa ke kigere k’ubwirwa ni izi zikurikira: inzira mbonankubone,
ibaruwa, telefoni, insakazamajwi, insakazamashusho, interineti.

252

2. Shushanya indanguruzi y’ubwumvane
Ikivugwaho
Ubutumwa/
Ingambo

Uvuga

Ubwirwa

Inzira/ umuyoboro
Inkurikizo

3. Vuga abanditsi b’ibi bihangano :
Matabaro ajya i Burayi (Kagame Alegisi)
Iyo mbimenya Niyitegeka (Mukarugira Yuliyana)
Hagati y’isi n’ijuru (Gasimab Faransisiko Saveri)

Imyitozo nyagurabushobozi

1. Hanga umwandiko mugufi (utarenze imirongo makumyabiri), ukubiyemo
imimaro y’ururimi, werekane buri mumaro uwucaho akarongo kandi
uwusobanure.
Urugero rw’igisubizo:
Nshuti bavandimwe, ndabamenyesha ko Igihugu cyacu cyugarijwe n’indwara
kirimbuzi. Nta yindi ni ebola (1). Niba mutayizi, ni indwara iterwa na virusi
itaramenyekana neza, yaturutse mu bihugu by’amahanga kandi ikaba yica cyane, nta
n’urukingo igira. Yadutse ahagana mu wa 2005 ikaba kandi imaze kuyogoza ibihugu
bitari bike (2). Ku bwange rero ndumva ibintu bikomeye ku buryo dukwiye gufata
ingamba hakiri kare (3) ejo tutazisama twasandaye(4). Muranyumva? (5) Kandi rero
nta we irebera izuba, ari umuto, ari umukuru, mbese twese, nange w’umuganga,
iratwibikiye nitutirinda (6). Mukenyere mukomeze rero, kandi ngo agapfa kaburiwe
ni impongo (7).

Ibisobanuro

1. Umumaro nyakivugwa (utanga ikiganiro aratanga amakuru y’imvaho).
2. Umumaro ndengarurimi (arasobanura ikintu akeka ko abamwumva
badasobanukiwe cyangwa batamenyereye, ijambo rikomeye: ebola).
3. Umumaro nsesekaza cyangwa nsesekazamutima (aragaragaza icyo atekereza
ku byo avuze yerekana imbamutima kuri iriya ndwara itinyitse).
4. na (7) umumaro nyabusizi cyangwa nyaburanga (aragerageza gukoresha
imvugo inoze, ashaka kuvuga neza, akoresha ikeshamvugo, imvugo
idasanzwe).

253

5. Umumaro nzira cyangwa nyakuzo (arabaza ariko mu by’ukuri nta gisobanuro
ategereje; ni ukugira ngo gusa yumve niba abantu bamukurikira).
6. Umumaro nyakubwirwa (arashaka kumvisha abamukurikiye ko ibyo avuga,
ni ukuvuga ububi bwa ebola, na bo bibareba, badakwiye kuba ba ntibindeba)
2. Sobanura umumaro nkurikizo (nyanteramvugo) unatandukanye uburyo
wumvikanamo. Umumaro nkurikizo (nyanteramvugo): Ni ibimenyetso byose
bisobanura icyo ubutumwa bwerekejeho. Bamwe bawita kandi umumaro
ndengarurimi cyangwa nsobanuzi.

254

9

Ibidukikije

(Umubare w’amasomo: 14)

Ubushobozi bw’ingenzi bugamijwe:
–
–

Gusesengura umwandiko uvuga ku kamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba.
Gusobanura imiterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda.

Ubumenyi bw’ibanze
Kugira ngo umunyeshuri atangire kwiga ibikubiye muri uyu mutwe yagombye kuba azi
akamaro k’ibidukikije muri rusange ashobora kandi gusobanura poritiki y’iby’indimi mu
Rwanda.
Ingingo nsanganyamasomo zivugwaho.
Kubungabunga ibidukikije: Ni ngombwa ko umwarimu asesengura ku buryo bwimbitse
umwandiko “Amazi n’amashyamba” ku buryo umunyeshuri asobanukirwa neza akamaro
ko kubungabunga amazi n’amashyamba.
Ubushobozi nsanganyamasomo
Ubushishozi no gushakira ibibazo ibisubizo, gusabana mu rurimi rw’Ikinyarwanda,
ubushakashatsi.
Amagambo fatizo: Ibidukikije, ururimi rw’igihugu, ururimi rw’ubutegetsi, ururimi
kavukire, inshami z’Ikinyarwanda, indimi z’uturere.
Uburyo bwo kwita ku bafite ibibazo byihariye: Umwarimu akora ku buryo yandika
ku kibaho imyitozo ikorwa kugira ngo abafite ubumuga bwo kutumva bayisome. Asoma
mu ijwi riranguruye yegereye kugira ngo abatumva neza n’abafite bumuga bwo kutabona
babyumve. Ni ngombwa kandi gufasha ku buryo bwihariye abafite ibibazo byihariye.

Ibice bikubiye muri uyu mutwe n’umubare n’amasomo ya buri gice
Igice
Umwandiko : Amazi n’amashyamba
Intera ya mbere : Gusoma no kumva umwandiko
Intera ya kabiri : Gusesengura umwandiko
Intera ya gatatu : Umwitozo w’ubumenyi ngiro: Kungurana
ibitekerezo
Intera ya kane: Imiterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda
Intera ya gatanu : Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda
Isuzuma
