Amasomo 2

Umwandiko: Amazi n’amashyamba
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 177)

Intera ya mbere: Gusoma no kumva umwandiko
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusoma umwandiko yubahiriza utwatuzo n’iyitsa.
– Gusobanura amagambo akomeye ari mu mwandiko.
– Kwitabira gukoresha neza mu mvugo no mu nyandiko amagambo yungutse.
– Gusubiza ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri kirimo umwandiko ku bidukikije, igitabo
cy’umwarimu, amashusho mvumburamatsiko n’inkoranyamagambo.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwitegereza amashusho ari mu gitabo
cy’umunyeshuri ahari umwandiko bagiye gusoma ababaze ibibazo bibafasha
kumva icyo asobanura no kuvumbura ibikubiye mu mwandiko yifashishije
ayo mashusho. Usibye ayo mashusho yo mu gitabo, umwarimu ashobora no
kwifashisha izindi mfashanyigisho zifatika cyangwa andi mashusho ajyanye
n’umwandiko akabaza ibibazo biganisha ku mwandiko bagiye gusoma

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza aramutse akoresheje amashusho:
a) Ni iki mubona kuri aya mashusho? Turahabona ishyamba n’ibikoresho bibajwe
mu mbaho.
b) Murabona ikirere kimeze gite hejuru y’ishyamba? Imvura irimo kugwa.
c) Ku bwanyu murumva uyu mwandiko uri buvuge ku ki? Uri buvuge ku kamaro
k’amashyamba.
Nyuma yo gusubiza ibyo bibazo cyangwa ibindi yababaza biganisha ku mwandiko
bagiye kwiga, umwarimu arahishurira abanyeshuri ko bagiye gusoma umwandiko
uvuga ku bidukikije.

2. Gusoma umwandiko
2.1

Gusoma bucece

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kurambura igitabo cyabo ahari umwandiko
“Amazi n’amashyamba”, abasabe kuwusoma bucece nyuma ababaze ibibazo
byo gusuzuma ko basomye.

256

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Abaturage bo mu majyaruguru ya Afurika bajya i Burayi banyuze ahantu
hameze hate? Banyura ahantu h’ubutayu.
b) Vuga amazina y’abagize umuryango uvugwa mu mwandiko wimukiye i
Burayi. Ni umugabo Gahigi, umugore we Mutesi n’abana babo babiri Nyiramana
w’umukobwa na Kamana w’umuhungu.
c) Uyu muryango ucagashije urugendo byawugendekeye bite? Umugabo Gahigi
yahise yitaba Imana.
d) Abaturage bamaze kwambuka ubutayu bahembuwe n’iki? Bahembuwe n’amazi
banyoye.
e) Ubutayu bariya baturage bambutse bwatewe n’iki? Bwatewe no gufata nabi
amashyamba bayatema ntibongere gutera ibindi biti.

2.2 Gusoma baranguruye

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko wose baranguruye.
Umunyeshuri umwumwe arajya asoma igika kimwe, abandi bakurikira mu
bitabo byabo. Nyuma yo kurangiza igika umwarimu asaba undi munyeshuri
kumusimbura bityo bityo kugeza umwandiko wose urangiye. Mu gihe umwarimu
ashakisha umunyeshuri usoma, arajya anagenzura uko bitabira gushaka gusoma.
Mu gihe basoma aragenda abakosora aho basomye nabi amagambo cyangwa aho
batubahiriza neza utwatuzo.

2.3 Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda

Barangije gusoma baranguruye, umwarimu ashyira abanyeshuri mu matsinda
anyuranye. Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri bagize buri tsinda kwishakamo umwe
ujya yandika ibisubizo bumvikanyeho muri buri tsinda. Akanabasaba gusoma
umwandiko no gushakishiriza hamwe ibisobanuro by’amagambo badasobanukiwe
kandi basubiza n’ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Umwarimu agabanya amatsinda ibibazo biri mu bitabo byabo, itsinda rimwe
rigafata ibibazo bibiri cyangwa bitatu bitewe n’umubare w’abanyeshuri
n’uw’amatsinda yakoze.
Umwarimu agendagenda mu matsinda agenzura uko abanyeshuri bakorera
hamwe, agatanga ubufasha ku babumusabye.
Nyuma y’iki gikorwa, umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kumurika ibyavuye muri buri
tsinda akabafasha kunonosora ibisubizo, bakabyandika ku kibaho no mu makayi
yabo.

257

Urugero rw’ibisubizo bishobora kuva mu matsinda
I. Inyunguramagambo
Urugero rw’ibisubizo bishobora kuva mu matsinda.
a) Sobanura amagambo akurikira:
1. Ibizazane: ibibazo bitandukanye, ingorane zizitira umuntu kugira icyo
ageraho.
2. Basaranganya: basangira uduke abantu bafite, basangira ibyo kurya bike
bari bafite.
3. Kuraba: kwara amaboko n’amaguru ukitura hasi ugahwera/ugapfa.
4. Arabacika: arapfa, yitaba Imana, avamo umwuka, aratabaruka.
5. Kurimbanya: kwihangana ugakomeza urugendo usa n’udafite imbaraga.
6. Gutora agatege: gutangira kugira akabaraga/ imbaraga.
7. Kwisunga abandi: kwegera abandi ngo mugire igikorwa mufatanya gukora.
8. Amapfa: icana cyane kw’izuba ibimera bikuma, amazi agakama mu bishanga
mu migezi n’izuzi.
9. Barasuhuka: bimukira, bava aho bari batuye bajya ahandi hantu.
10. Kugundira: kwimana ikintu ku buryo kukikwaka byavamo imirwano.
11. Ishyanga: imahanga, ahantu ha kure y’aho wari usanzwe utuye.
12. Igikingi cy’umuntu: umurima munini umuntu ahinga akawubyaza
umusaruro.
b) Umwitozo w’inyunguramagambo
Umwarimu abwira abanyeshuri kujya mu matsinda ya babiribabiri kugira ngo
bakore umwitozo uri mu bitabo byabo. Abagenera igihe bagomba gukora uwo
mwitozo, icyo gihe cyarangira akabayobora maze bagakosorera hamwe uwo
mwitozo. Umwe mu bagize itsinda rimwe ajya imbere akavuga yandika ku
kibaho ibyo itsinda ryabo ryakoze, nyuma umwarimu n’abandi banyeshuri
bakanononsora ibisubizo byatanzwe bikandikwa ku kibaho no mu makayi. Ibyo
bisubizo kandi babisoma mu ijwi riranguruye kugira ngo n’abatabona ku kibaho
babyumve.
Urugero rw’umwitozo n’ibisubizo:
Simbuza ijambo ryanditse ritsindagiye irindi bivuga kimwe dusanga mu
mwandiko, wite ku mategeko ngengasano mu nteruro nshya wubatse.
1. Amapfa yateye ikama ry’ibiyaga n’ibishanga.
2. Amage arisha umugabo ikivuza, inzara se ntiyatumye dusuhuka tukerekeza i
Bushi.
3. Amazi yarabuze mu mudugudu wacu umwuma utuma benshi batakaza ubuzima.
4. Gahigi na Mpwerazikamwa bamaze iminsi bafashe agatege basubiye mu kibuga.

258

5.
6.
7.
8.

Amazi n’amashyamba bidufatiye runini, ntawarota yangiza ibyo bidukikije.
Amashyama n’amazi ni intaho y’inyamaswa n’udukoko dutandukanye.
Amadovize dusigirwa na ba mukerarugendo afasha Igihugu muri byinshi.
Ku mbibi z’Igihugu cyacu mu burengerazuba hari ikiyaga cya Kivu.

II. Ibibazo n’ibisubizo byo kumva umwandiko

1. Ni ubuhe butayu abimukira bambukaga buri mu majyaruguru ya
Afurika? Ubutayu abimukira bambuka buri mu majyaruguru ya Afurika ni
ubutayu bwa Sahara.
2. Mu mwandiko batubwira ko abo bimukira bahungaga amapfa. Ese
amapfa aterwa n’iki? Amapfa ashobora guterwa no kuva kw’izuba ryinshi
ry’igihe kirekire cyangwa se imvura nyinshi.
3. Ni gute ibyo bari bitwaje byarangiriye mu nzira kandi barahagurutse
biteguye bihagije kandi bazi ko urwo rugendo ruzamara igihe
kinini? Bageze mu nzira bahura n’abandi bimukira batari biteze, bagenda
basaranganya ibyo bari bafite kandi bajya kwitegura ntibari bazi umubare
w’abo baza guhura na bo mu rugendo rwabo.
4. Abo mu muryango wa Gahigi babuze ubushobozi bwo kumuramira
arapfa. Ukeka ko ari ubuhe bushobozi buvugwa aha? Ubushobozi
buvugwa ni ubwo kumubonera amazi yo kunywa ndetse no kubona aho
bugama agacucu.
5. Ni akahe kamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba kagaragara mu mwandiko?
Amazi aranyobwa, amazi acumbikiye udukoko n’inyamaswa zaremewe kuba
mu mazi, amazi atuma imyaka duhinga yera. Amashyamba avamo ibikoresho
bitandukanye, atanga ibiti abantu bubakisha, ni indiri y’inyamaswa,
amashyamba ayungurura umwuka duhumeka, akurura imvura, avamo imiti
ituvura, afata ubutaka, ni isoko y’ubukerarugendo…
6. Ni gute imigezi n’inzuzi bifatiye runini abaturiye isi? Imigezi n’inzuzi
byubakwaho ingomero zitanga umuriro w’amashanyarazi akoreshwa mu
nganda, mu itumanaho n’ikoranabuhanga.
7. Vuga nibura inyamaswa eshatu zitaha mu manshyamba? Imbogo, intare,
ingwe, n’izindi.
8. Ni ibihe biti waba uzi bivamo imiti gakondo? Umuravumba, umusave,
umuko, umubirizi, igikakarubamba, umutagara, umuhoko, umwenya, …
9. Ni ibihe biti biduha imbuto turya? Avoka, amatunda, amapera, ibinyomoro,
imyembe, n’ibindi
10. Imbuzo duhabwa n’ibyo biti zigirira akahe kamaro umubiri wacu?
Imbuto z’ibyo biti ziturinda indwara, zikavamo na vitamin zikomeza uruhu
rwacu.
11. Umwandiko ujya kurangira batubwira ko wa mukobwa yamenye ikishe
abo mu muryango we? Utekereza ko yavumbuye ko bishwe n’iki? Bishwe
no kubura amazi yo kunywa.

259

Intera ya kabiri: Gusesengura umwandiko
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 180)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusubiza neza ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba ashobora kubungabunga amazi
n’amashyamba.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri n’igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
zivuga ku bidukikije.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo bigamije ku mwibutsa umwandiko
baheruka gusoma bakavuga muri make n’ibyari biwukubiyemo.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Ni uwuhe mwandiko duheruka gusoma? Amazi n’amashyamba
b) Wavugaga kuki muri rusange? Wasobanuraga akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba,
uburyo amashyamba akurura imvura, ayungurura umwuka duhumeka, uburyo
amazi amara umwuma, uburyo atuma ibihingwa byera,...
Nyuma yo gusubiza ibyo bibazo, umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri kurambura ibitabo
byabo ahari umwandiko? Amazi n’amashyamba.

2. Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda no kuwusesengura

Umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko bari mu matsinda no
kugerageza kuwusesengura bagaragaza insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko
n’ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwamo. Umwarimu araha abanyeshuri ibibazo bibafasha
kuvumbura insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko n’ibindi bituma bagaragaza
ingingo z’ingenzi ziri mu mwandiko. Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko
arabivuga mu ijwi riranguruye anabyandike ku kibaho kugira ngo afashe abatumva
niba bahari.

Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko:

1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko?
2. Vuga ingingo z’ingenzi ziri mu mwandiko.
3. Abantu benshi muri Afurika basuhukira i Burayi. Urakeka ko bose baba bajyanwe
no gushaka amaramuko?

260

4. Nutema kimwe uge utera bibiri. Iyi mvugo ifite ishingiro? Sobanura ku buryo
bwumvikana.
5. Hina umwandiko wasomye mu mirongo itarenze icumi.

Ibisubizo:

1. Insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko ni akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba.
2. Ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwa muri uyu mwandiko ni izi zikurikira:
– Itera ry’amapfa.
– Isuhuka ry’abaturage bo muri Afurika y’amajyaruguru.
– Urupfu rutewe n’umwuma rwa bamwe mu bari basuhutse.
– Kuramirwa n’amazi kuri bamwe bari bagifite akuka.
– Iyangiza ry’amashyamba ryateye amapfa.
– Impamvu tugomba kubungabunga amazi n’amashyamba
– Impamvu tugomba kubungabunga ibidukikije muri rusange.
3. Abasuhukira i Burayi bose ntibaba bajyanywe no gushaka amaramuko. Hari
abagenda bagiyeyo ku mpamvu za politiki z’ubuhunzi, abandi bajyayo basanga
bene wabo kwiberayo gusa kuko bumva bavuga ngo i Burayi ni heza. Yumvikane
neza ko hari n’abajyayo bagiye gutembera bazagaruka.
4. Iyi mvugo ifite ishingiro koko. Niba utemye igiti jya wihutira gutera ikindi kuko
bitabaye ibyo, buri wese akitemera ibiti uko yishakiye, wazasanga isi isigaye
yambaye ubusa bikatugiraho ingaruka kuko amashyamba adufatiye runini nk’uko
twabibonye mu mwandiko.
5. Aha umwarimu areba niba mu guhina umwandiko umunyeshuri yagaragaje mo
ingingo zose z’ingenzi kandi akaba yubahirije uburebure bwasabwe.

Intera ya gatatu: Kungurana ibitekerezo
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupapuro rwa 180)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasesenguye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusobanura uburyo bwo kubungabunga ibidukikije.
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba agaragaza imyitwarire yo
kubungabunga ibidukikije.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
ku bidukikije.

261

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu arabaza abanyeshuri umwandiko baheruka gusesengura bamubwire
ko ari umwandiko ujyanye n’akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba. Umwarimu arabwira
abanyeshuri ko bagiye kuganira ku buryo bwo kubungabunga ibidukikije.

2. Kungurana ibitekerezo

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo
ugaragara mu gitabo cy’umunyeshuri aranabasaba kuwandika ku kibaho kugira
ngo afashe abatumva neza kuwubona no kuwusobanukirwa.

Umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo

“Ganirira na bagenzi bawe muvuga ku buryo bwo kubungabunga ibidukikije buri
mu mwandiko n’ubutagaragara mu mwandiko.”
Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwishakamo umuyobozi w’ikiganiro
nyunguranabitekerezo. Uwo arajya aha abandi umwanya wo gutanga ibitekerezo
byabo no kugira icyo yavuga ku bitekerezo by’abandi mu bwubahane. Mu gihe
bungurana ibitekerezo umwarimu agenzura uko abanyeshuri bitabira gutanga
ibitekerezo kugira ngo abatabyitabira abibashishikarize. Nyuma yo kungurana
ibitekerezo umwarimu afatanya n’abanyeshuri gukora umwanzuro ku byo
bunguranyeho ibitekerezo. Bikandikwa ku kibaho.
Urugero rw’igisubizo gishoboka:
Uburyo bunyuranye bwo kubungabunga ibidukikije:
– Gutera amashyamba;
– Gukorera amashyamba;
– Kurwanya isuri bacukura imiringoti;
– Kutamena imyanda mu mazi;
– Kudatwika amashyamba;
– [...]

Intera ya kane: Imiterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 181)
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku bushakashatsi yakoze, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Kuvuga inshoza y’ururimi rw’igihugu n’ururimi rukoreshwa mu butegetsi.
– Gutandukanya inshoza y’ururimi rw’igihugu n’iy’urukoreshwa mu butegetsi
n’indimi kavukire no kugaragaza uko byifashe mu Rwanda.
– Gusobanura inshoza y’imvugo z’uturere, kuzirondora no kwerekana uturere
zivugwamo.

262

Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’igitabo
k’iyigandimi.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma igika cy’umwandiko baheruka gusoma
nyuma akabasaba gusubiza ibibazo bigikurikiye.

Urugero rw’igika basoma n’ibibazo basubiza:
[...]
Bamaze kugera mu bihugu by’i Burayi bahahurira n’indi mbogamizi y’ururimi. Nubwo
bamwe na bamwe bari baravuye mu gihugu kimwe ntibashoboraga kumvikana kubera
indimi z’uturere twabo zabaga zitandukanye. Abashoboraga kumvikana ni ababaga
bavuga ururimi rumwe ariko nabwo bakaruvuga mu buryo bunyuranye kubera
ko uturere bakomokagamo twabaga dutandukanye. Bakigera i Burayi bari barize
Igifaransa n’Icyongereza kuko ari zo zari indimi z’ubutegetsi mu bihugu bakomotsemo,
bumva ko bazumvikana neza n’abaturage b’i Burayi nyamara si ko byagenze, basanze
Igifaransa n’Icyongereza na byo bifite izindi ndimi zibishamikiyeho ari na zo abaturage
baho bivugiraga. Uko imyaka yahitaga ni ko barushagaho kugenda bumvikana ubwabo
ndetse bakanumvikana na ba kavukire bo mu bihugu basuhukiyemo.

Ibibazo:

Ururimi rw’igihugu ni iki? Ururimi kavukire ni iki? Ururimi rw’ubutegetsi n’iki?
Ururimi rw’akarere ni iki? Kora ubundi bushakashatsi bwimbitse ku miterere
y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda ugaragaze n’aho zivugirwa ku ikarita.

2. Isesengura

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gukorera ubushakashatsi mu matsinda bagerageza
gusubiza ibyo bibazo byabajijwe no gukora ubundi bushakashatsi bwimbitse ku
miterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda. Abaha igihe cyo kubikora cyagera akabasaba
kuzaza kumurika ibyavuye mu matsinda akabafasha kubinonosora bigakorerwa
inshamake ikandikwa ku kibaho no mu makayi yabo.

Urugero rw’ibyava mu bushakashatsi:

1. Inshoza y’ururimi nyagihugu

Ururimi nyagihugu ni ururimi igihugu kiba kihariye ubwacyo kandi rukaba
rwemewe n’amategeko. Zishobora no kuba nyinshi nko muri Repubulika Iharanira
Demokarasi ya Kongo. Inshoza y’ururimi nyagihugu mu bihugu bimwe na bimwe
yitiranywa n’iy’ururimi rw’ubutegetsi kubera ko akenshi ururimi nyagihugu

263

rukoreshwa mu mishyikirano n’ubutegetsi n’umuco.
Mu Rwanda ururimi nyagihugu ni Ikinyarwanda mbonera.
Ingero:
Abanyarwanda bavuga Ikinyarwanda, Abarundi bavuga Ikirundi…

2. Inshoza y’ururimi kavukire

Ubusanzwe tuvuga ko kanaka avuga ururimi kavukire iyo tumugereranya n’uvuga
ururimi nyagihugu rufatwa nk’imvugo mbonera cyangwa ururimi mbonera kandi
rwo rukaba rufite ingufu. Icyo gihe imvugo kavukire ifatwa nk’imvugo itanoze,
akenshi ikaba isuzugurwa nk’imvugo y’abantu bake cyangwa se imvugo izingiye
ahantu hamwe kandi hato cyane. Akenshi imvugo kavukire ikoreshwa iyo bavuga.
Ni ururimi rw’aho ukomoka, ni ururimi wavukiyemo ruvugwa n’ababyeyi, ba
sogokuru, ba nyogokuru, ba nyogokuruza, ingoma ibihumbi. Mu yandi magambo
ni ururimi gakondo.
Urugero:
Mu Rwanda Ikinyarwanda ni ururimi kavukire rw’Abanyarwanda benshi.

3. Inshoza y’ururimi rw’ubutegetsi

Ururimi rw’ubutegetsi ni ururimi ruteganywa mu itegeko nshinga ry’igihugu,
rugakoreshwa mu nyandiko z’ubuyobozi cyangwa z’imiryango inyuranye ikorera
mu gihugu. Ni ururimi rukoreshwa mu nyandiko zose z’ubuyobozi, mu nkiko,
mu mabanki n’ahandi.
Urugero:
Ubu Rwanda indimi z’ubutegetsi ni enye: Ikinyarwanda, Igifaransa, Icyongereza
n’Igiswayiri.

4. Inshoza y’ururimi rw’akarere

Ururimi rw’akarere ni ururimi ruturuka ku mihindagurikire y’ururimi nyagihugu
bitewe n’imiryango y’abantu cyangwa akarere abaruvuga baherereyemo.
Urugero:
Ikigoyi n’Ikirera ni indimi z’akarere zikoreshwa mu majyaruguru.

5. Ikinyarwanda n’imvugo z’uturere

Mu Rwanda abantu benshi bahuzwa n’ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda. Ikinyarwanda
ni rumwe mu ndimi nyafurika bita Indimi Bantu cyangwa Nyantu. Izo ndimi
zivugwa munsi y’ubutayu bwa Sahara. Izo ndimi nyantu iyo uzisesenguye neza
usanga zifite byinshi zihuriyeho. Duhereye nko ku Giporotobantu cyari gifite
inteko z’amazina makumyabiri n’enye, zaje kugabanywamo cumi n’esheshatu,
bityo ugasanga n’Ikinyarwanda cyarafashe izo nteko cumi n’esheshatu cyongeraho
izindi eshatu ziranga ahantu, zose hamwe ziba cumi n’ikenda.

264

Imvugo z’uturere n’aho zivugwa
Abasesenguye indimi basanze ururimi rutavugwa kimwe ku butaka ruvugwaho.
Akarere kamwe kagenda kagira umwihariko wako w’imivugire haba mu majwi,
mu masaku, mu magambo no mu buryo bwo kubaka interuro. Uko ni ko n’ururimi
rw’Ikinyarwanda rutavugwa kimwe mu Rwanda hose. Iyo uzengurutse u Rwanda
usanga hari imvugo z’uturere zigera kuri eshanu. Reka turebere hamwe izo mvugo
z’uturere uko zihagaze mu Rwanda.
a) Ikigoyi: Kivugwa mu gice cy’u Rwanda kiri hagati y’ishyamba rya Gishwati
n’Igihugu cya Repubulika Iharanira Demokarasi ya Kongo: Kanama, Nyamyumba
na Rubavu. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rubavu.
b) Ikirera: Kivugwa mu cyahoze ari Ruhengeri hose ukuyemo Ndusu n’akandi gace
gato kavugwamo Igikiga. Ikirera kandi kivugwa mu duce twa Mutura, Giciye,
Karago n’agace gato ka Cyungo. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rubavu.
c) Igikiga: Ni imvugo ivanga Ikirera n’imvugo y’ahahoze ari Gitarama (Akarere
ka Kamonyi na Ruhango). Igikiga kivugwa kandi ahahoze ari Gaseke, Satinsyi,
Kibirira, Ramba, Rutsiro, Kayove na Nyakabanda. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngororero.
Uretse Nyakabanda iri mu Ntara y’Amajyepfo, utwo duce twose turi mu Ntara
y’Iburengerazuba. Igikiga kandi kivugwa mu gace ka Mwendo na Gisovu
(Kibuye) no ku ishyamba rya Nyungwe mu duce twa Nshiri, Kivu, Mudasomwa
na Musebeya. (Ubu ni mu Karere Karongi)
d) Igisozo: Kivugwa mu gice giherereye hagati y’ishyamba rya Nyungwe n’u Burundi
n’umuhanda uhuza Nyakabuye Bugarama (Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi). Igisozo
n’igikiga bitangiye gucika kubera Ikinyarwanda rusange. Ntibyoroshye kandi
gutandukanya igikiga n’igisozo.
e) Ikinyarwanda cyo hagati: Nk’uko kitwa, kiganje mu gice cyose cyo hagati mu
Gihugu. Ikinyarwanda cyo hagati ni cyo Kinyarwanda rusange kuko ari cyo
cyagizwe ururimi rwigwa mu mashuri, rugakoreshwa mu itangazamakuru no
mu butegetsi kugira ngo Abanyarwanda bose barusheho kumvikana bakoresheje
imvugo imwe.
f) Ikigoti: Ni ururimi rukoreshwa ku nkiko z’amagepfo mu Karere ka Gisagara mu
Murenge wa Mugombwa.
g) Igishobyo n’Ikiyaka: Izi ndimi zikoreshwa mu karere kamwe ahahoze ari Kanama
na Nyamyumba. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rubavu.
h) Urunyagisaka n’Urunyambo: Izi ndimi zikoreshwa ahahoze ari Perefegitura ya
Kibungo. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngoma.

265

Umwitozo

Nyuma y’isomo umwarimu aha abanyeshuri umwitozo wo gusuzuma ko intego
z’isomo zagezweho. Uyu mwitozo ukorwa na buri munyeshuri ku giti ke. Bagahuriza
hamwe ibisubizo umwarimu akabafasha kubinonosora.
Urugero rw’mwitozo n’igisubizo
Ni izihe ndimi z’ubutegetsi zikoreshwa mu Rwanda? Ni Ikinyarwanda, Igifaransa,
Icyongereza n’Igiswayiri.

Intera ya gatanu: Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda
(Igitabo cy’umunyesuri)

Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku bushakashatsi yakoze, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusobanura inshami z’Ikinyarwanda n’aho zikoreshwa mu Gihugu.
Imfashanyigisho: Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’igitabo
k’iyigandimi.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko ugaragara mu bitabo byabo,
nyuma akabasaba gusubiza ibibazo bigakurikiye.

Urugero rw’akandiko basoma n’ibibazo basubiza:
Uzi ko Kankera ari nge nyoko ukubyara. Kera twari dutuye i Bushi, sogokuru mu
burengerazuba bw’u Rwanda, ubu ni muri Repubulika Iharanira Demukarasi ya Kongo
yahoze yitwa Zayire. Twe n’abavandimwe bacu bo ku Kirwa cya Nkombo no ku
nkengero za Kivu, twavugaga Amashi, Amahavu; aho tugereye hano mu majyaruguru
byaratugoye kumvikana n’abaturage bo muri iyi midugudu, ndetse iyo tugannye mu
isoko hari ubwo bidutesha umutwe!
Tukiba i Bushi wasangaga tuvuga tuti: “Hari omusale hari ehisale”. Ugira ngo se
ntitwashakaga kuvuga ko hari umusare n’ingirwamusare! Umusare w’umwuga
yakwambutsaga akujyana ku Ijwi ukishima ariko ba bandi b’abiga hari n’ubwo
batamenyaga ikerekezo cy’umuyaga, umuhengeri ukaba wabateza akaga gakomeye.
Hano ku Mulindi w’intwari twahimukiye bavuga ngo: “Ebirungyi ni ho byeija,
rwombeke rugume, ogumire nk’eibare...” nkayoberwa icyo bashatse kuvuga, ariko ubu
nta cyo bambeshya! None se Ceri, wari uzi icyo izina ryawe risobanura? Twakubyariye
hano ku Mulindi tukwita dutyo!

266

Cerimpa bahimba Macibiri yasubije nyina atishisha. Nyina ntiyari azi ko Macibiri
yamenye kera igisobanuro k’izina rye. Yasubije mu magambo akurikira: icyo kibazo ni
cyo mwarimu yambajije nkitangira mu mashuri y’inshuke. Nzi ko nitwa Ikimanimpaye,
ureke urwo Luciga mwantwereye! Masenge Nyiramatwi yanambwiye ko aho batuye
ku Rusumo, ku mbibi z’u Rwanda na Tanzaniya mu burasirazuba, bavuga Ikinyambo;
twe tuvuga Oluciga atwita Abakiga bavangiye kuko tutahavuka. Muzamusabe kwirinda
kuvangura abantu, ibyo bizanshimisha cyane!
Ibibazo
Izi ndimi ko atari Ikinyarwanda nk’uko dusanzwe tukizi, zaba zifitanye iyihe sano na
cyo? Sobanura igisubizo utanze.
Kora ubushakatsatsi bwimbitse ku ndimi zifitanye isano n’Ikinyarwanda ugaragaza
aho zivugwa.

2. Isesengura

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gukorera ubushakashatsi mu matsinda bagerageza
gusubiza ibyo bibazo byabajijwe no gukora ubundi bushakashatsi bwimbitse
ku nshami z’Ikinyarwanda. Abaha igihe cyo kubikora cyagera akabasaba
kuzaza kumurika ibyavuye mu matsinda akabafasha kubinonosora bigakorerwa
inshamake ikandikwa ku kibaho no mu makayi yabo.

Urugero rw’ibyava mu bushakashatsi:

Inshoza y’ururimi shami

Uretse Ikinyarwanda, hari izindi ndimi nyafurika zishamikiye kuri uru rurimi
rw’Ikinyarwanda. Izi ndimi zishamikiye ku Kinyarwanda usanga ahanini ziganje ku
nkiko z’Igihugu cyacu. Bene izi ndimi usanga zifite isano ya bugufi n’Ikinyarwanda.
Zimwe muri izo ndimi shami z’Ikinyarwanda ni izi zikurikira:
a) Oluciga: Mu Rwanda Oluciga ruvugwa n’abantu benshi ariko by’umwihariko
ruvugwa cyanecyane mu duce duherereye mu majyaruguru y’u Rwanda ahahoze
ari Komini Kiyombe, Muvumba, Cyumba, Kivuye, Mukarange na Butaro. Ubu
ni mu Karere ka Gicumbi na Burera. Uru rurimi kandi ruvugwa mu gihugu
cy’abaturanyi, cy’u Bugande.
b) Amashi n’Igihavu cyangwa Amahavu: Abazi izi ndimi bavuga ko zijya gusa cyane.
Izi ndimi zivugwa mu burengerazuba bw’u Rwanda ku mupaka wa Repubulika
Iharanira Demukarasi ya Kongo n’u Rwanda. Igihavu kivugwa ku Kirwa cya
Nkombo no ku nkombe z’Ikiyaga cya Kivu ahahoze ari muri komini Gisuma,
Gafunzo na Kagano. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi na Nyamasheke. Amashi yo
avugwa mu gice cyo hepfo y’Ikiyaga cya Kivu. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi.
c) Ururashi: Ururashi bamwe bita Urunyambo ruvugwa mu burasirazuba bw’u
Rwanda mu bice bimwe na bimwe by’ahahoze ari Komini Rusumo na Rukira ku
mupaka w’u Rwanda na Tanzaniya. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngoma.

267

Ikitonderwa
N’ubwo abatuye aho hantu bavuga izo ndimi batandukaniraho n’abandi, bafite ururimi
rusange rubahuza n’abandi ari rwo rw’Ikinyarwanda uzasanga rukoreshwa mu masoko
bahaha cyangwa se mu nama zitandukanye no mu biganiro byahuje abantu.

Umwitozo

Nyuma y’isomo umwarimu aha abanyeshuri umwitozo wo gusuzuma ko intego
z’isomo zagezweho. Uyu mwitozo ukorwa na buri munyeshuri ku giti ke. Bagahuriza
hamwe ibisubizo umwarimu akabafasha kubinonosora.
Urugero rw’umwitozo n’igisubizo
1. Andika mu nshamake imiterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda. Indimi zo mu
Rwanda zigabanyijemo ibice bibiri by’ingenzi: indimi shami z’Ikinyarwanda
n’imvugo z’uturere . Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda n’indimi zihariye ziganje ku mipaka.
Twavugamo nka Oluciga, Amashi, Igihavu, n’Ururashi. Imvugo z’uturere zo ni
umwihariko w’imivugire uba ushingiye ku majwi, ku masaku, mu magambo no mu
misusire y’interuro . Dusangamo Igisozo, Igikiga, Ikirera, Ikigoyi n’Ikinyarwanda
cyo hagati mu Rwanda ari na cyo gihuriweho n’Abanyarwanda bose kandi
gikoreshwa mu butegetsi, mu itangazamakuru, mu nama no mu masoko.
2. Ushingiye ku miterere y’indimi mu Rwanda, vuga ibice by’u Rwanda bivugwamo
izi ndimi cyangwa izi mvugo: Oluciga, Ururashi, Ikirera n’Amashi.
– Oluciga ruvugwa mu majyaruguru.
– Ururashi ruvugwa mu burasirazuba.
– Ikirera kivugwa mu majyaruguru.
– Amashi avugwa mu burengerazuba.

Inshamake y’ibikubiye muri uyu mutwe wa kenda

Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo binyuranye bituma bashobora kuvuga
iby’ingenzi bikubiye mu mutwe wa kenda. Ibyo bibazo yababaza bishobora gutuma
batanga inshamake ikurikira:

– Amazi n’amashyamba bidufatiye runini. Amashyamba akurura imvura
akayungurura umwuka duhumeka, avamo ibikoresho bitandukanye, hari ibiti
bivamo imiti ituvura. Ikindi ni uko amashyamba acumbikiye inyamaswa n’utundi
dukoko dutandukanye. Amazi atanga ingufu, acumbikira inyamaswa ziba mu
mazi, turayoga, aratekeshwa, aranyobwa, muri make amazi ni ubuzima.
– Abanyarwanda bose si ko bavuga kimwe ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda. Buri karere
kagenda kagira umwihariko wako w’imivugire, haba mu ijwi, mu masaku, mu
magambo mu buryo bwo kurema interuro n’ibindi. Kubera iyo mpamvu, mu
Rwanda hari indimi zishamikiye ku Kinyarwanda zikoreshwa n’Abanyarwanda.

268

– Mu Rwanda indimi zikoreshwa mu butegetsi ni Ikinyarwanda, Icyongereza,
Igifaransa, n’Igiswayiri. Indimi z’uturere mu Rwanda ni Ikigoyi, Ikirera, Igikiga,
Igisozo, Ikigoti, Igishobyo, Ikiyaka, Urunyagisaka n’Urunyambo.
– Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda ni Oluciga, Amashi, n’Ururashi.

Ubumenyi bw’inyongera
Izindi nshoza z’ingenzi zijyanye n’imiterere y’iby’indimi
–
–
–
–

–

Imvugo bwite: Ni uburyo umuntu runaka yihariye bwo kuvuga ururimi. Mu
Kinyarwanda tubyita kuvuga uburimi.
Urwuga cyangwa urunyagatsiko: Ni imvugo iba yihariwe n’itsinda ry’abantu
bahuje umwuga cyanecyane mu rwego rw’amagambo n’imivugire. Bityo habaho
urwuga rw’abanoteri, abacengerandimi, abahanga mu kurira imisozi, n’abandi.
Urufefeko: Urwuga rwihariye ruvugwa n’abantu badasanzwe kandi barangwa
no kwironda nk’udutsiko tw’abajura babigize umwuga cyangwa abari mu mitwe
y’iterabwoba. Bahitamo gukoresha ururimi rwihariye rutakumvwa na bose.
Ivangandimi: Urujya n’uruza hagati y’imiryango nyarurimi ivuga indimi
zitandukanye rutuma havuka ururimi mvangitirane rutuma abantu bumvikana
kandi ntibakenere isemura. Ururimi ruvamo iyo rudafite amategeko arugenga ni
rwo bita « Igisabiri », naho rwagira amategeko arugenga rukitwa «Igipigini». Iyo
rubaye ururimi rw’ibanze rw’umuryango nyarurimi runaka rwitwa «Igikerewore»
Ubuvuganyinshi: Bavuga ko umuntu cyangwa itsinda ry’abantu ari abavuganyinshi
iyo bashobora gukoresha indimi zirenze ebyiri.

Ibibazo n’ibisubizo by’isuzuma rusange risoza umutwe wa kenda
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)

Ibigenderwaho mu isuzuma:

– Ubushobozi bwo gusubiza ibibazo ku mwandiko yasomye no guhanga
umwandiko ku nsanganayamatsiko yahawe.
– Ubushobozi bwo gutandukanya Ikinyarwanda, indimi shami n’indimi z’uturere.

Uyu mwitozo ukorwa n’umunyeshuri ku giti ke nyuma yawo umwarimu aramukosora
akareba ibibazo byihariye buri munyeshuri afite kugira ngo arebe uko yamufasha
mbere yo gutangira undi mutwe.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva no gusesengura umwandiko

1. Kera abantu bafataga isi dutuye bakayigabanyagamo inturo cyangwa
ibice, abantu, inyamaswa n’inyoni bibarizwaho. Bivuge usobanura.
		– Mu gihugu k’inyoni: mu bushorishori bw’ibiti.
		 – Mu bwami bw’inkuba: mu kirere hejuru y’ibimera byose.

269

		
		

–
–

Igihugu cy’umwami Nyirurwanda: isi ituwe n’abantu, inyamaswa n’ibintu.
Mu gihugu k’ifuku: mu nsi y’ubutaka, aho inyamaswa zimwe zigira
icumbi mu myobo.
2. Vuga akamaro k’ibimera.
		 – Bivamo imiti ikoreshwa mu buvuzi,
		 – Bishobora kuvamo ibiribwa,
		 – Bitanga imbuto ziribwa,
		 – Bicumbikira inyoni n’inyamaswa,
		 – Bitanga ibiti byubakishwa amazu,
		– Bikurura imvura,
		– Bituma haboneka umwuka abantu bahumeka,…
3. Mu bidukikije harimo ibintu bibiri birusha ibindi ingufu. Bivuge. Amazi
n’amashyamba.
4. Amashyamba akomeye avugwa mu mwandiko ni ayahe? Amashyamba
akomeye avugwa mu mwandiko ni Icyanya cya Nyungwe, Mukura,
Cyamudongo, Gishwati, Icyanya k’Akagera n’Icyanya cy’Ibirunga.
5. Ibimera bibuze twaba mu butayu. Vuga ubutayu bwatanzweho ingero
mu mwandiko. Ubutayu butangwaho ingero ni Ubutayu bwa Karahali,
n’ubwa Sahara.
6. Rondora ingaruka zaterwa no kubura amazi.
		 – Ibimera n’ibihingwa byakuma,
		 – Amatungo n’inyamaswa byapfa kubera kubura ibyo birya,
		 – Abantu bapfira gushira,
		– Ubukungu bwayoyoka,…
7. Abantu tugomba kwamagana bangiza amashyamba n’inyamaswa ni ba
nde? Abantu tugomba kwamagana ni ba rutwitsi n’abashimusi tutibagiwe
n’abahigi.
8. Ingingo nyamukuru n’izungirije zavuzweho mu mwandiko zigaragaze.
		 Ingingo nyamukuru n’izungirije:
		 – Akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba,
		 – Inyamaswa dusanga mu mashyamba,
		 – Ibimera bitanga ibiribwa,
		– Akamaro k’imvura,
		– Akamaro k’amashyamba,
		 – Kwamagana ba rutwitsi n’abashimusi,
		 – Uko tugomba kubungabunga ibidukikije.
9. Huza ubuzima bwawe cyangwa bwacu muri rusange n’amashyamba
		 – Amashyamba ayungurura umwuka duhumeka,
		 – Amashyamba atanga ibiti bubakisha amazu,
		 – Avamo ibikoresho bitandukanye,

270

		 – Avamo imiti itandukanye ivura abantu,
		 – Bimwe mu biti biribwa n’inyamaswa,..
10. Ni izihe ngamba zafatwa kugira ngo hakomeze kubungabungwa
ibidukikije?
		 – Igihe utemye igiti kimwe jya wibuka gutera bibiri cyangwa ibirenzeho,
		 – Guhinga udatengurira itaka mu biyaga, inzuzi n’imigezi,
		 – Gutera ibiti birinda isuri ku migezi, ku biyaga n’inzuzi,
		 – Gukorera ubukangurambaga abangiza amashyamba, amazi n’inyamaswa,
		 – Guca amatarasi ndinganire no guteraho ibyatsi birinda isuri,…

II. Inyunguramagambo

a) Shaka ibisobanuro by’aya magambo akurikira ukurikije uko
yakoreshejwe mu mwandiko umaze gusoma.
		1. Ahakinje: ahicaye, ahari, ahaherereye.
		2. Tuyasazure: turimbure ibiti bishaje dutere ibindi bishya.
		3. Ibikwa: ibiti bahagararaho bubaka inzu kugira ngo badatemba, batagwa
hasi.
		4. Ba tereriyo: ba bandi batagira icyo bitaho, ba ntibindeba.
		5. Kuba akamizwe n’ingwe: kuzimira ku buryo abantu baheba burundu.
		6. Imperuka: umunsi ibintu byose bizarangiriraho, umunsi w’irangira
ry’isi.
		7. Gushya ururimbi: gushya ibintu bigakongoka.
		8. Baraducukurira urwobo: baratwifuriza gupfa, baradukururira
amakuba, ibyago.
		9. Inkongi y’umuriro: umuriro utwika amashyamba, amapariki,
amashyamba,...
		10. Mu manegeka: ahantu habi hahanamye abantu batagombye gutura.
b) Simbuza ijambo cyangwa itsinda ry’amagambo atsindagiye irindi/andi
bivuga kimwe dusanga mu mwandiko.
		1. Akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba ni ntarondoreka.
		2. Rwabwiga mwita umwami w’ishyamba irivuga abantu bagakangarana.
		3. Rwarikamavubi zifitanye isano cyane n’inka zororwa n’Abanyarwanda
n’abandi bantu batuye iyi si.
		 4. Utazi akamaro k’ikintu aragikerensa.
		5. Aya marebe yangiza udukoko dusanzwe dutorera iyo mu mazi cyane
cyane amafi.
c) Uzuza interuro zikurikira ukoresheje ijambo rikwiye ukuye mu
mwandiko.
		1. Dusazure amashyamba turimbura ibiti bishaje dutera ibishya.
		 2. Abiba inyamaswa ari bo ba RUshimusi ntibagomba kwihanganirwa.

271

		 3. Ibiriho byose bizashira ku munsi w’imperuka hasigare urukundo.
		4. Indarama zirura kurusha imitanga.

III.Iyigandimi
Tandukanya ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda, indimi shami n’indimi z’uturere
utanga n’urugero.
Ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda ni ururimi nyagihugu rwigishwa mu mashuri kandi
rugendera ku mategeko y’ikibonezamvugo azwi. Indimi z’uturere indimi ziba zihariwe
n’abantu bo mugace runaka ariko na zo ari Ikinyarwanda. Indimi shami ni indimi
zifitanye isano ya bugufi n’Ikinyarwanda.

Imyitozo nzamurabushobozi
–
–

Umwarimu atahura ikigero cy’ubushobozi bwa buri munyeshuri ahereye ku
manota bagize mu isuzuma risoza umutwe akabashyira mu matsinda akurikije
ingorane bafite.
Umwarimu abaha imyitozo ijyanye n’ibyiciro byabo bakayikorera hamwe mu
matsinda. Ku badafite ibibazo, umwarimu abavanga muri ya matsinda y’abafite
ibibazo kugira ngo bafashe abandi gusobanukirwa neza n’imyitozo bakora
cyangwa se akabasaba gukora imyitozo nyagurabushobozi. Iyi myitozo umwarimu
ayitegura ashingiye ku bibazo abanyeshuri be bafite yibanda aho abanyeshuri be
bagaragaje ubushobozi buke.

Imyitozo y’inyongera
1. Ni izihe ngamba wafata kugira ngo hakomeze kubungabungwa ibidukikije?
– Igihe utemye igiti kimwe jya wibuka gutera bibiri cyangwa ibirenzeho,
– Guhinga udatengurira itaka mu biyaga, inzuzi n’imigezi,
– Gutera ibiti birinda isuri ku migezi, ku biyaga n’inzuzi,
– Gukorera ubukangurambaga abangiza amashyamba, amazi n’inyamaswa,
– Guca amatarasi ndinganire no guteraho ibyatsi birinda isuri, n’ibindi.
2. Ushingiye ku bumenyi wungutse wasobanura ute imvugo yitwa «Urucuzi»
n’«Urushumba»?
Urucuzi ni ururimi nyamuryango ruhuriweho n’abantu bahuje umwuga w’ubucuzi
naho urushumba ni urufefeko cyangwa urunyagatsiko kuko barukoresha bashaka
ko abatari mu gatsiko kabo batumva ibyo bavuga.

272

10

Ibiyobyabwenge

(Umubare w’amasomo: 21)

Ubushobozi bw’ingenzi bugamijwe:
–

Gusesengura umwandiko ku biyobyabwenge no kwandika neza inyandiko z’ubutegetsi,
amatangazo anyuranye n’ubutumire.

Ubumenyi bw’ibanze
Kugira ngo umunyeshuri atangire kwiga ibikubiye muri uyu mutwe yagombye kuba azi
bimwe mu biyobyabwenge kandi ashobora gutandukanya inyandiko zisanzwe n’inyandiko
z’ubutegetsi.
Ingingo nsanganyamasomo zivugwaho.
Umuco w’ubuziranenge: Ni ngombwa ko umwarimu asesengura ku buryo bwimbitse
umwandiko yigwamo kugira ngo umunyeshuri asobanukirwe ko ibiyobyabwenge biba
bitujuje ubuziranenge, ko ari ngombwa kubyirinda.
Ubushobozi nsanganyamasomo
Ubushishozi no gushakira ibibazo ibisubizo, gusabana mu rurimi rw’Ikinyarwanda,
ubushakashatsi…
Amagambo fatizo: Ibiyobyabwenge, ibaruwa isanzwe, ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi, umwirondoro,
itangazo.
Uburyo bwo kwita ku bafite ibibazo byihariye: Umwarimu akora ku buryo yandika ku
kibaho imyitozo ikorwa kugira ngo abafite ubumuga bwo kutumva bayisome. Asoma mu
ijwi riranguruye yegereye kugira ngo abatumva neza n’abafite ubumuga bwo kutabona
babyumve. Ni ngombwa kandi gufasha ku buryo bwihariye abafite ibibazo byihariye.

Ibyigwa bikubiye muri uyu mutwe n’umubare n’amasomo ya buri gice
Ikigwa
Umwandiko : Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge, imungu y’ubuzima
bwacu
Intera ya mbere : Gusoma no kumva umwandiko
Intera ya kabiri : Gusesengura umwandiko
Intera ya gatatu : Umwitozo w’ubumenyi ngiro: Kungurana
ibitekerezo no kujya impaka
Intera ya kane: Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi n’umwirondoro
Intera ya gatanu : Amatangazo n’ubutumire
Isuzuma

Umubare w’amasomo
Amasomo 4
Amasomo 2
Amasomo 2
Amasomo 5
Amasomo 5
Amasomo 3

Umwandiko: Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge, imungu y’ubuzima bwacu
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 190)

Intera ya mbere: Gusoma no kumva umwandiko
Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusoma umwandiko yubahiriza utwatuzo n’iyitsa.
– Gusobanura amagambo akomeye ari mu mwandiko.
– Kwitabira gukoresha neza mu mvugo no mu nyandiko amagambo yungutse.
– Gusubiza ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri kirimo umwandiko ku bidukikije, igitabo
cy’umwarimu, amashusho mvumburamatsiko n’inkoranyamagambo.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwitegereza amashusho ari mu gitabo
cy’umunyeshuri ahari umwandiko bagiye gusoma ababaze ibibazo bibafasha
kumva icyo asobanura no kuvumbura ibikubiye mu mwandiko yifashishije
ayo mashusho. Usibye ayo mashusho yo mu gitabo, umwarimu ashobora no
kwifashisha izindi mfashanyigisho zifatika cyangwa andi mashusho ajyanye
n’umwandiko akabaza ibibazo biganisha ku mwandiko bagiye gusoma

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza aramutse akoresheje amashusho:
a) Ni iki mubona kuri aya mashusho? Turahabona abantu bari kunywa inzoga
n’itabi.
b) Murabona abo bantu bameze bate? Barasa n’abasinze.
c) Ku bwanyu murumva uko bagaragara byatewe n’iki? Byatewe no kunywa
inzoga nyinshi.
d) Uducupa barikunywa twanditseho iki? Twanditseho kanyanga.
e) Kanyanga ni ikinyobwa bwoko ki? Ni ikiyobyabwenge.
Nyuma yo gusubiza ibyo bibazo cyangwa ibindi yababaza biganisha ku mwandiko
bagiye kwiga, umwarimu arahishurira abanyeshuri ko bagiye gusoma umwandiko
uvuga ku biyobyabwenge.

274

2. Gusoma umwandiko
2.1. Gusoma bucece

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kurambura igitabo cyabo ahari umwandiko
“Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge, imungu y’ubuzima bwacu”, abasabe kuwusoma
bucece nyuma ababaze ibibazo byo gusuzuma ko basomye.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Muri uyu mwandiko baratubwira ko ikiyobyabwenge ari iki? Ni ikintu cyose
iyo kinjiye mu mubiri w’umuntu gihindura ubushobozi bwe bwo gutekereza,
gufata ibyemezo ndetse kikanahindura imikorere y’umubiri.
b) Ni izihe ndwara z’umubiri zivugwa mu mwandiko zishobora guterwa
n’ibiyobyabwenge? Indwara z’umwijima, kanseri, indwara z’ubuhumekero,...
c) Ni izihe nzungano zivugwa muri uyu mwandiko? Haravugwamo urwungano
ngogozi n’urwungano rw’imyakura.

2.2. Gusoma baranguruye

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko wose baranguruye.
Umunyeshuri umwumwe arajya asoma igika kimwe, abandi bakurikira mu
bitabo byabo. Nyuma yo kurangiza igika umwarimu asaba undi munyeshuri
kumusimbura bityo bityo kugeza umwandiko wose urangiye. Mu gihe umwarimu
ashakisha umunyeshuri usoma, arajya anagenzura uko bitabira gushaka gusoma.
Mu gihe basoma aragenda abakosora aho basomye nabi amagambo cyangwa aho
batubahiriza neza utwatuzo.

2.3. Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda

Barangije gusoma baranguruye, umwarimu ashyira abanyeshuri mu matsinda
anyuranye. Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri bagize buri tsinda kwishakamo umwe
ujya yandika ibisubizo bumvikanyeho muri buri tsinda. Akanabasaba gusoma
umwandiko no gushakishiriza hamwe ibisobanuro by’amagambo badasobanukiwe
kandi basubiza n’ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko.
Umwarimu agabanya amatsinda ibibazo biri mu bitabo byabo, itsinda rimwe
rigafata ibibazo bibiri cyangwa bitatu bitewe n’umubare w’abanyeshuri
n’uw’amatsinda yakoze.
Umwarimu agendagenda mu matsinda agenzura uko abanyeshuri bakorera
hamwe, agatanga ubufasha ku babumusabye.
Nyuma y’iki gikorwa, umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kumurika ibyavuye muri buri
tsinda akabafasha kunonosora ibisubizo, bakabyandika ku kibaho no mu makayi
yabo.

275

Urugero rw’ibisubizo bishobora kuva mu matsinda
I. Inyunguramagambo
Urugero rw’ibisubizo bishobora kuva mu matsinda.
a) Sobanura amagambo akurikira:
1. Indangagaciro: umuco umuntu cyangwa abantu baba bafite utuma
bashobora kwitwara neza mu bandi.
2. Ubumuntu: kamere muntu.
3. Akanyabugabo: agashema umuntu yigiramo kakamutera agasumbwe
4. Muriture: ubwoko bw’izoga y’inkorano
5. Bareteta: ubwoko bw’izoga y’inkorano
6. Urwungano rw’imyakura: urwungano rw’ibijyanye n’ubwonko.
7. Impagarara: ibintu by’ibizira byaduka ku muntu cyangwa mu bantu benshi
bigatuma babura amahoro.
8. Intandaro: inkomoko y’ikintu runaka
9. Kwadukira: kwibasira bwa mbere
10. Gusenyera umugozi umwe: kumvikana mu byo mukora byose.
b) Umwitozo w’inyunguramagambo
Umwarimu abwira abanyeshuri kujya mu matsinda ya babiribabiri kugira ngo
bakore umwitozo uri mu bitabo byabo. Abagenera igihe bagomba gukora uwo
mwitozo, icyo gihe cyarangira akabayobora maze bagakosorera hamwe uwo
mwitozo. Umwe mu bagize itsinda rimwe ajya imbere akavuga yandika ku
kibaho ibyo itsinda ryabo ryakoze, nyuma umwarimu n’abandi banyeshuri
bakanononsora ibisubizo byatanzwe bikandikwa ku kibaho no mu makayi. Ibyo
bisubizo kandi babisoma mu ijwi riranguruye kugira ngo n’abatabona ku kibaho
babyumve.
Urugero rw’umwitozo n’ibisubizo:
1. Simbuza ijambo cyangwa itsinda ry’amagambo yanditse atsindagiye irindi/
ayandi magambo bivuga kimwe
a) Ibiyobyabwenge birimo ibice byinshi: ibiterwa umuntu mu rushinge,
ibinyobwa nk’itabi, hari ibisukika n’ibindi. (→ingeri, ibyiciro)
b) Abantu bari mu kigero kimwe bagomba gukorera hamwe mu bwumvikane
n’amahoro. (→urungano)
c) Itsinda ry’abantu badatunganye ntiwagombye kubishinga kuko bakuyobya
(→agatsiko).
d) Uwaheranywe n’ibitotsi ubuticura iyo atitaweho ashobora kugendera ko
agashiramo umwuka. (→itiro)

276

2. Uzuza interuro zikurikira ukoresheje ijambo rikwiye ukuye mu mwandiko.
a) Umuntu wabaye imbata y’ibiyobyabwenge usanga yarataye umutwe.
b) Umubiri w’umunywi w’ibiyobyabwenge utakaza ubushobozi byo kunoza
umurimo.
c) Isaho umwana yiberamo igihe nyina amutwise yitwa ingobyi, igomba
kubungwabungwa ku ikubitiro.
d) Abasaritswe n’ibiyobyabwenge baba mu bukene butava ku muryango.

II. Ibibazo n’ibisubizo byo kumva umwandiko

1. Vuga impamvu enye abantu bitwaza bakishora mu biyobyabwenge?
		 Bishora mu biyobyabwenge bitwaje ko bari gushaka akanyamuneza
n’akanyabugabo, gusabana n’urungano, gushakisha ibitotsi, gutuma umuntu
atinyuka akagira ingufu n’ubushyuhe mu mubiri.
2. Tanga ingero z’ibiyobyabwenge bikunze gukoreshwa mu gace
ukomokamo. Hakunze gukoreshwa kanyanga n’urumogi. (Aha abanyeshuri
batanga ibisubizo binyuranye.)
3. Garagaza ibikorwa binyuranye abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge bashobora
kwishoramo igihe bamaze kubikoresha. Bakora urugomo, ubujura,
guhohotera abo bahuye na bo bose bityo ubwicanyi bwa hato na hato ndetse
n’amakimbirane adashira bikadogera.
4. Ni izihe ndwara zishobora gukomoka ku ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge?
Uburwayi bw’umwijima, umutima, ubwonko n’imyakura, ubumuga,
uburwayi bw’urwungano ngogozi, indwara z’ubuhumekero, iz’ibihaha ku
banywi, kanseri, SIDA, n’ibindi.
5. Ni gute gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge bishobora kuba intandaro yo
kwandura SIDA? Sobanura ukoresheje ingero ebyiri. Hari ibiyobyabwenge
bafata bakoresheje urushinge rumwe ku bantu batandukanye bikaba
byabaviramo kwandura SIDA. Rimwe na rimwe ukoresha ibiyobyabwenge,
kubera kutagira uwo atinya n’icyo atinya, ashobora kwishora mu ngeso
z’ubusambanyi atiteguye bityo bikaba byamuviramo kwandura indwara
z’ibyorezo nka SIDA kuko akenshi akora imibonano mpuzabitsina idakingiye
akayikorana n’abo atazi kandi banyuranye.
6. Ni ryari ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge rishobora kuganisha ku rupfu?
Ni igihe urugero rw’ibiyobyabwenge rwiyongereye cyane mu maraso.
7. Ni ibihe bimenyetso biranga uwagizwe imbata n’ibiyobyabwenge?
Ikiranga uwo ibiyobyabwenge byagize imbata ni uko akenera kongera
ingano y’ibiyobyabwenge kugira ngo yumve amerewe neza uko abishaka.
Atakaza ishyaka ryo gukora ibindi bintu bitari ikoresha ry’ibiyobyabwenge,
mbese agakoresha igihe kinini cy’umwanya we wa buri munsi ku ikoreshwa
ry’ibiyobyabwenge. Akomeza gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge n’igihe azi neza
ingorane cyamuteje n’ingaruka ashobora guhura na zo. Iyo bene abo

277

bantu batakoresheje ibiyobyabwenge bakunze kurangwa no kugira ishavu,
imihangayiko, gucika intege, kwiheba, kwiheza n’ibindi.
8. Usanzwe akoresha ibiyobyabwenge arangwa n’iki igihe yabibuze?
Abakoresha ibiyobyabwenge iyo batabikoresheje bakunze kurangwa no
kugira ishavu, imihangayiko, gucika intege, kwiheba, kwiheza n’ibindi.
9. Ibiyobyabwenge bibangamira iterambere ry’igihugu kigaragaramo
ababikoresha cyane. Sobanura uburyo bibangamira iterembere
wifashishije ingero ebyiri. Abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge batakaza
amafaranga menshi bayasesagura mu biyobyabwenge bikaviramo
imiryango yabo ubukene bw’akarande. Mu rwego rw’ubukungu kandi
ibihugu bikoreshwamo cyane ibiyobyabwenge bisabwa gushora amafaranga
menshi mu miti aho kuyashora mu bindi bikorwa by’iterambere bitewe no
kurwaragurika kw’abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge n’ababakomokaho. Abanywi
b’ibiyobyabwenge bica akazi bikadindiza iterambere
10. Ugendeye ku mwandiko, sobanura impamvu tugomba kurwanya
ibiyobyabwenge.
		
Tugomba kubirwanya kubera ko bigira ingaruka ku buzima bw’ababikoresha,
bigira ingaruka ku iterambere ry’umuryango no ku iterambere ry’igihugu
muri rusange.
11. Uyu mwandiko ni iki ushishikariza ababyeyi bafite abana biga mu
mashuri yisumbuye?
		 Urabashishikariza kwita ku bana babo babashishikariza kwamagana
ababashora mu biyobyabwenge no kubibutsa ingaruka zabyo.
12. Ushingiye ku bivugwa muri uyu mwandiko, ni iyihe nama wagira
bagenzi bawe? Kubera iki? Nabagira inama yo kwirinda ibiyobyabwenge
no kubyamagana. Kubera ko bigira ingaruka zitandukanye ku ubikoresha,
ku muryango we no ku iterambere ry’igihugu muri rusange.

Intera ya kabiri: Gusesengura umwandiko
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 195)

Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasomye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusubiza neza ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko.
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba ashobora gushishikarira no
gushishikariza abandi kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri n’igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
zivuga ku biyobyabwenge.

278

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo bigamije ku mwibutsa umwandiko
baheruka gusoma bakavuga muri make n’ibyari biwukubiyemo.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Ni uwuhe mwandiko duheruka gusoma?
Ni umwandiko “ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge, Imungu y’ubuzima bwacu”.
(b) Wavugaga kuki muri rusange? Wasobanuraga ibiyobyabwenge ukagaragaza
n’ingaruka zabyo zinyuranye.
Umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri ko bagiye gusesengura umwandiko baheruka
gusoma abasabe kongera kuwusomera mu matsinda.

2. Gusomera umwandiko mu matsinda no kuwusesengura

Umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri gusoma umwandiko bari mu matsinda no
kugerageza kuwusesengura bagaragaza insanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko
n’ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwamo n’ibindi bibazo. Ibibazo byo gusesengura
umwandiko arabivuga mu ijwi riranguruye anabyandike ku kibaho kugira ngo
afashe abatumva niba bahari. Arasaba buri tsinda gusubiza ibyo bibazo no kuza
kumurika ibisubizo byaryo umwarimu abafashe kubinonosora byandikwe ku
kibaho.

Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko n’ibisubizo bishoboka:

1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko rusange ivugwa muri uyu mwandiko? Uyu
mwandiko uravuga ku biyobyabwenge n’ingaruka zabyo.
2. Garagaza ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwa muri uyu mwandiko.
– Icyo ibiyobyabwenge ari cyo.
– Ikiciro cy’abantu gikunze kugaragaramo ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge.
– Ingaruka zo gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge ku muntu ubikoresha.
– Ingaruka zo gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge ku muryango we.
– Ingaruka zo gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge ku iterambere ry’igihugu.
– Impamvu tugomba kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge.
3. Ibiyobyabwenge birangiza. Abaporisi n’abandi bashinzwe umutekano
bashoboye bonyine kubirwanya no kubikumira? Gerageza kubisobanura
neza. Ntabwo bashobora kubirwanya bonyine ni ngombwa ko habaho ubufatanye
bwa buri wese mu kubirwanya.
4. Abantu batari bake usanga bafata ku gahiye ndetse bagasingira ka Wisiki
n’izindi nzoga zikomeye, hari aho bihuriye no kunywa ibiyobyabwenge
byarondowe mu mwandiko? Nta ho bihuriye kuko ibiyobyabwenge biba bitujuje
ubuziranenge ariko izo nzoga zo ziba zujuje ubuziranenge ariko si buri wese
uba yemerewe kuzinywa, biterwa n’imyemerere ye ndetse n’ikigero ke, n’igihe
azinywera n’icyo akora.

279

5. Mu mashuri yisumbuye, mu mashuri makuru na za Kaminuza usanga hari
ubwisanzure bukabije ku buryo kugenzura ko banywa ibiyobyabwenge
bitakorohera uwo ari we wese. Abayobozi b’ayo mashuri wabifuzaho iki?
Nabagira inama yo gukaza umurego mu kubirwanya bagashakisha izindi ngamba
zo gukurikirana abo bashinzwe.

Intera ya gatatu: Kungurana ibitekerezo
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)

Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku mwandiko yasesenguye, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusobanura uruhare rw’ibiyobyabwenge mu kudindiza iterambere.
– Nyuma y’iri somo kandi umunyeshuri azaba agaragaza imyitwarire yo kurwanya
ibiyobyabwenge no kubishishikariza abandi.
– Gutinyuka kuvugira mu ruhame.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu n’izindi nyandiko
ku biyobyabwenge.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Isubiramo

Umwarimu arabaza abanyeshuri umwandiko baheruka gusesengura bamubwire
ko ari umwandiko ujyanye n‘ibiyobyabwenge. Umwarimu arabwira abanyeshuri
ko bagiye kuganira ku bubi bw’ibiyobyabwenge mu iterambere.

2. Kungurana ibitekerezo

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo
ugaragara mu gitabo cy’umunyeshuri aranabasaba kuwandika ku kibaho kugira
ngo afashe abatumva neza kuwubona no kuwusobanukirwa.

Umwitozo wo kungurana ibitekerezo

“Ifatanye na bagenzi bawe mwungurane ibitekerezo mujya impaka zubaka kuri iyi
nsanganyamatsiko: Uruhare rw’ibiyobyabwenge mu kudindiza iterambere n’ingamba
zo kubyirinda.”
Umw ar imu a sab a ab anye shur i k w i shak amo umuyob oz i w ’ik ig aniro
nyunguranabitekerezo. Uwo arajya aha abandi umwanya wo gutanga ibitekerezo byabo
no kugira icyo yavuga ku bitekerezo by’abandi mu bwubahane. Mu gihe bungurana
ibitekerezo umwarimu agenzura uko abanyeshuri bitabira gutanga ibitekerezo kugira
ngo abatabyitabira abibashishikarize. Nyuma yo kungurana ibitekerezo umwarimu
afatanya n’abanyeshuri gukora umwanzuro ku byo bunguranyeho ibitekerezo.
Bikandikwa ku kibaho.

280

Urugero rw’igisubizo gishoboka:
Uruhare rw’ibiyobyabwenge mu kudindiza iterambere:
– Kurwara indwara zinyuranye zigatuma abarwaye badakorera ingo zabo;
– Abanywa ibiyobyabwenge Leta ibatangaho amafaranga menshi yagombye
kujyanwa mu bindi bikorwa remezo;
– Abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge basesagura amafaranga ntibarihire abana babo mu
mashuri;
– [...]
Ingamba:
– Kurwanya ihingwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge no kwenga kanyanga.
– Gukumira icuruzwa ry’ibiyobyabwengenge ku mashuri ndetse n’ahandi hose.
– Abantu bose kandi bakwiye gukomeza kurwanya uburaya, ubuzererezi
n’ubwomanzi mu migi.
– Gukomeza ibihano bisanzwe biteganywa n’amategeko no gushyira imbaraga mu
biganiro kuri radiyo na televiziyo, mu binyamakuru no mu nama zinyuranye
hakaba no gutanga ubuhamya ku babaye mu buzima bwo kunywa ibiyobyabwenge.
– Gukomeza ubufatanye hagati y’inzego no gushyiraho uburyo bumwe
bw’ikusanyamibare iturutse hirya no hino.
– Gukangurira abakuru n’abato kuzibukira ibiyobyabwenge, guhiga bukware
ababicuruza, ibifashwe bigatwikwa ku mugaragaro, ababinywa bafashwe
bagahanwa by’intangarugero. Rubanda kandi bashishikarizwa gutungira
ubuyobozi agatoki ahaba haherereye ibiyobyabwenge hose.
– […]

Intera ya kane: Ibaruwa n’umwirondoro
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 196)

Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku bushakashatsi yakoze, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusobanura uturango tw’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi n’umwirondoro.
– Kwandika ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi n’umwirondoro.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu, amabaruwa
y’ubutegetsi anyuranye n’imyirondoro y’abantu banyuranye.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri gusoma igika kivuye mu mwandiko baheruka
gusoma maze akababazaho ibibazo biganisha ku isomo baheruka kwiga.

281

Urugero rw’igika n’ibibazo yababazaho :
By’umwihariko, kubera impungenge baterwa n’ibiyobyabwenge bishobora no
kwadukira amashuri yisumbuye, ababyeyi ntibahwema gushishikariza abana babo
kwamagana ababashora mu biyobyabwenge binyuranye ndetse no kubibutsa ingaruka
zabyo ku buzima bwabo bw’ejo hazaza. Rimwe na rimwe ababyeyi bandikira abana
babo amabaruwa babasaba kwirinda ibiyobyabwenge.
Urugero rw’ibibazo n’ibisubizo:
1. Ibaruwa ivugwa muri iki gika ni bwoko ki? Ni ibaruwa ya gicuti (isanzwe).
2. Usibye bene ubwo bwoko bw’ibaruwa nta bundi bwoko bw’ibaruwa waba uzi?
Tanga ingero zitandukanye. Hari kandi ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi (y’ubuyobozi).
3. Mu matangazo y’akazi ni ki bakunze gusaba? Basaba ibaruwa isaba akazi
n’umwirondoro urambuye.
4. Utekereza ko umwirondoro uba ukubiyemo iki? Uba ukubiyemo inyandiko
imuranga ibyo yize n’ibyo yakoze
Umwarimu araboneraho kubwira abanyeshuri ko bagiye kwiga ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
n’umwirondoro abasabe kurambura ibitabo byabo ahari iyo baruwa n’umwirondoro.

2. Isesengura

Muri iki gice umwarimu arasaba abanyeshuri kujya mu matsinda anyuranye
amatsinda amwe ayasabe gusoma ibaruwa igaragara mu bitabo byabo, andi
ayasabe gusoma umwirondoro. Nyuma y’icyo gikorwa arabasaba gusubiza ibibazo
bibajijwe munsi y’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi no munsi y’umwirondoro anabasabe
gukora ubundi bushakashatsi bwimbitse batahura inshoza n’uturango n‘imbata
by’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi n’umwirondoro.
Amatsinda yahaye gukora ku ibaruwa arabanza amurike ibyo yagezeho narangiza
amatsinda yahaye gukora ku mwirondoro arakurikiraho. Umwarimu arafatanya
n’andi matsinda kunonosora ibyavuye mu bushakashatsi bw’ayo matsinda.

Urugero rw’ibyava mu matsinda:

Ibibazo n’ibisubizo ku ibaruwa:

1. Uyu mwandiko uri mu buhe bwoko bw’imyandiko? Kubera iki? Ni ibaruwa
kubera ko ugaragaza ibice by’ibaruwa.
2. Ni nde wanditse uyu mwandiko? Yandikiye nde? Ni Mugisha Arnaud, yandikiye
umuyobozi w’Umurenge wa Kagano.
3. Ni ikihe kibazo cyatumye nyiri ukwandika uyu mwandiko awandika? Ni
ikibazo k’ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge.
4. Garagaza isano iri hagati y’uyu mwandiko n’uwo uherutse gusoma no
gusesengura. Nta sano namba bifitanye, biratandukanye cyane.

282

Ibyava mu bushakashatsi:
1. Inshoza y’ibaruwa y‘ubutegetsi

Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ni ibaruwa ngufi kandi ivuga ibya ngombwa birasa ku
ntego, ikirinda uburondogozi no kugaragaza amarangamutima. Igituma iba
ngufi ni uko uwandika agomba gusa kwibanda ku mpamvu yatumye yandika.
Bene iyi baruwa y’ubutegetsi iba igamije gusaba akazi, gusubiza uwasabye akazi,
gusaba ibisobanuro mu kazi, gutanga ibisobanuro, gutanga amabwiriza n’ibindi.
Tuvuge niba uwandika asaba akazi, iyo ni yo mpamvu agaragaza ku rupapuro
kandi ni yo avugaho muri make agaragaza akazi ashaka ako ari ko, ubushobozi
afite bwo kugakora n’uburyo azagakora, kandi byose bikagirwa mu kinyabupfura.
Nta gutandukira ngo agaragaze ko yababaye, ko ubukene bumumereye nabi, ko
arya rimwe mu cyumweru, ko yari afite akazi bakakamwirukanaho ku maherere
n’ibindi. Uwo wandikira usaba akazi ntakeneye ko umurondogoraho cyangwa
ko umutera imbabazi. Ibyo nta mwanya abifitiye, icyo akeneye ni icyo ugamije
kumukorera, ubushobozi ugifitemo, ibyangombwa bibigaragaza. Bene iyi baruwa
yandikwa ku rupapuro rw’umweru bita A4, rukandikwaho ruhagaze, uwandika
agahera ibumoso agana iburyo.

2. Imiterere y’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi

Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi igizwe n’ibice binyuranye:

Aderesi: Ni igice k’ingenzi kigaragaza uwanditse ibaruwa. Hagaragaramo amazina
ye, aho atuye ndetse n’andi makuru yose yafasha uwo yandikiye kumenya aho
yamubariza aramutse amushatse. Ashobora kongeramo nimero za terefoni
n’aderesi ye ya interineti. Iki gice gifata umwanya wo hejuru ibumoso ku rupapuro.
Itariki: Ni ngombwa ko hagaragaramo ahantu ibaruwa yandikiwe n’umunsi iyo
baruwa yandikiweho. Iki gice cyo kijya hejuru iburyo ahateganye n’izina.
Uwandikiwe: Iki gice kigaragara munsi y’itariki ibaruwa yandikiweho. Kiba
kigaragaza uwo ibaruwa igenewe. Si izina rye bwite rigaragaramo ahubwo ni izina
rigaragaza umwanya afite mu kazi. Cyakora hashobora no kugaragazwa izina iyo
ibaruwa igenewe umukozi runaka.
Impamvu: Mu ibaruwa nk’iyo hagomba kugaragaramo impamvu yayo: gusaba
akazi, gusaba ibisobanuro n’ibindi. Ijambo impamvu buri gihe ricibwaho akarongo.
Iki gice kiba kiri munsi ya aderesi kikabangikana n’umurongo wa nyuma wo mu
gice kigaragaza uwo ibaruwa yandikiwe.
Ibaruwa nyirizina: Ni igice gikubiyemo ubutumwa bw’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
kigirwa n’ibika bitatu:

283

a) Intangiriro: uwandika avuga muri make impamvu imuteye kwandika igirwa
n’igika kimwe kandi ikagaragaza icyo uwandika agamije. Iyo ari nk’ibaruwa isaba
akazi agaragazamo ko azi neza ko uwo mwanya uhari yongeramo aho yabikuye;
mu kinyamakuru cyangwa mu itangazo ryaciye kuri radiyo.
b) Igihimba: Uwandika asobanura atarondogoye ingingo z’ingenzi zigize impamvu
yo kwandika. Ni igice kigaragara nk’aho ari kirekire kurusha ibindi, kuko
gishobora no kugira ibika birenze kimwe bitewe n’ingingo zigize ubutumwa. Ni
cyo gice cyonyine gisobanura mu mugambo arambuye ibyavuzwe mu ntangiriro,
kikabisesengura, kikanakurikiranya ibitekerezo. Icyo gihe buri gika kiharira
ingingo yacyo, na none ukirinda gusubiramo ibyo wavuze.
c) Umusozo: Uwandika ibaruwa asoza ashimira uwo yandikiye. Ni cyo gice kirangiza
ibaruwa kandi kigirwa n’igika kimwe. Uwandika arangiza yerekana ikizere afitiye
uwo yandikiye cyangwa se icyubahiro amugomba.
Amazina n’umukono: Ni igice gisoza ibaruwa kigizwe n’amazina ndetse n’umukono
wa nyiri ukuyandika.
Ikitonderwa: Bitewe n’imiterere yayo, ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ishobora kugira ibindi
bice bikurikira:
Binyujijwe kuri: Ni igice kigaragara mu ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi munsi y’aderesi
y’uwandikiwe. Gishyirwaho iyo hari abo iyo baruwa igomba gucaho mbere yo
kohererezwa uwayandikiwe.
Bimenyeshejwe: Ni igice kijya mu mpera z’ibaruwa ku ruhande w’ibumoso. Kijya mu
ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi iba igomba kugira abandi bamenyeshwa ibyanditswe.

284

3. Imbata y’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
Aderesi

285

4. Uturango tw’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
–

		
–

–
–

Hari amagambo yabugenewe agomba gutangira no gusoza ibaruwa
y’ubutegetsi.
(Bwana/Madamu Muyobozi, Nyakubahwa Minisitiri...)
Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ntawusuhuza cyangwa ngo asezere. Hari amagambo
yabugenewe asoza ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi (Mu gihe ngitegereje igisubizo
cyanyu, nyakubahwa, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mu gihe ntegerezanyije ikizere,
mbaye mbashimiye/ Mbaye mbashimiye Nyakubahwa/ Bwana / Madamu/
Madamazera…)
Buri gika gitangirira mu cya kabiri cy’urupapuro mu mpagarike yarwo.
Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi igomba kugira impamvu yayo yihariye bitewe
n’igitumye yandikwa.

Umukoro

Nyuma yo kumurika ibyavuye mu matsinda no kubinonosora bafatanije na mwarimu,
umwarimu aha abanyeshuri umukoro bakazawukorera mu rugo ugakosorwa mbere
yo gutangira isomo rizakurikiraho.
Urugero rw’umukoro n’uko wakosorwa:
“Andikira umuyobozi w’umurenge utuyemo umusaba ikemezo cy’amavuko kuko
ugikeneye mu kuzuza ifishi izaguhesha uburenganzira bwo gukora ikizamini cya Leta
gisoza amashuri yisumbuye. Wubahirize imbata y’ibaruwa nk’iyo.”
Umwarimu azareba niba abanyeshuri bubahirije uturango n’imbata by’ibaruwa
y’ubutegetsi afatanye na bo kujora zimwe na zimwe.

B. Umwirondoro

Ibibazo n’ibisubizo ku mwirondoro

1. Uyu mwandiko ugaragaramo ayahe makuru? Irangamimerere
– Amashuri
– Uburambe
– Ubundi bumenyi
– Indimi avuga...
2. Urabona ufite akahe kamaro? Ufite akamaro ko kwerekana ubushobozi bw’usaba
akazi.
3. Ni nka nde wakenera bene uyu mwandiko? Ni umukoresha ushaka abakozi.

Ibyava mu bushakashatsi:
A. Inshoza y’umwirondoro

Mu buzima busanzwe ari na byo bimenyerewe cyane, umwirondoro ukunze

286

gusabwa n’umuntu wese ushaka gutanga akazi. Bityo mu byangombwa yaka ushaka
gupiganirwa uwo mwanya haba harimo n’umwirondoro we. Umwirondoro ni
nk’inyandiko yereka umukoresha ishusho y’umukozi akeneye. Ntawakwirengagiza
ko umwirondoro ushobora kuba ngombwa iyo umuntu asaba ishuri runaka ngo
akomerezemo amasomo ye.

B. Ibiranga umwirondoro mwiza

Umwirondoro mwiza ugomba kuba:
– Wanditse ku rupapuro rwiza nta n’amakosa y’ururimi arimo.
– Wuzuye kuko uwusaba akeneye amakuru yuzuye kugira ngo arusheho
kumenya nyiri umwirondoro niba hari ikiburamo ntibizamutere igihe
agishakisha.
– Usomeka neza wanditswe mu nteruro ngufi.
– Uvuga ukuri. Ukora umwirondoro ntagomba kugira icyo yibagirwa cyangwa
ngo ashyiremo ibidasobanutse cyangwa ibihimbano.
– Ugomba kuba ugenewe koko abo wandikiwe, niba ari aho nyirawo asaba
akazi ugomba kuba ujyanye n’aho asaba akazi.
Umwirondoro ntukorwa uko nyirawo yiboneye ugomba kuba ufite uburyo buboneye
ukorwamo, uko ibice biwugize bikurikirana kuko umwanya wabyo uba ufite icyo
usobanura kuri uwo mwirondoro. Ibyo bice rero ni:
– Umutwe
– Ibiranga umuntu
– Amashuri
– Uburambe
– Ubundi bumenyi
– Indimi avuga
– Ibyo akunda
– Abantu bamuzi
– Kwemeza ko ari ukuri no gushyiraho umukono we.

1. Umutwe

Umutwe w’umwirondoro wandikwa hejuru ukitwa umwirondoro

2. Ibiranga umuntu

Irangamimerere ni igice gitangira umwirondoro, kikaba kigamije kugaragaza muri
make uwo ari we. Kigomba kuba cyumvikana kandi kirasa ku ntego. Si ngombwa
gushyiramo ibintu byinshi nubwo bwose waba ubona umwirondoro ari muto.
Mu irangamimerere umuntu avugamo amazina ye. Ni byiza kwandika izina
ry’umuryango mu nyuguti nkuru z’icyapa maze iry’idini rigatangizwa inyuguti
nkuru izindi zikaba nto. Nyuma y’amazina hagaragazwa aho umuntu aherereye,

287

ni ukuvuga aho atuye (aha iyo afite agasanduku k’iposita ni byiza kugashyiraho).
Aho umuntu atuye hiyongeraho n’uburyo uwamushaka yamubonamo; umurongo
wa terefoni na aderesi ya interineti ku buryo uwabishaka yahita amwandikira.
Ikindi kigomba kujya mu irangamimerere ni imyaka umuntu afite. Aha ariko
ntawandika umubare ibyiza ni ugushyiraho umwaka yavukiye. Iyo yanditse
amatariki, ukwezi akwandika mu izina ryako.

3. Amashuri

Iki gice kigaragaza aho nyiri umwirondoro ahagaze mu rwego rw’ubumenyi ni yo
mpamvu uwandika agomba guhera ku mpamyabumenyi nini afite. Mu kwandika
umwirondoro, amashuri ntatandukana n’impamyabumenyi, umuntu yandika
impamyabumenyi afite bikaba bihwanye no kwandika amashuri yize. Ugaragaza
amashuri yize avuga umwaka, aho yigaga, ibyo yigaga n’impamyabumenyi
yahakuye.
Hari igihe amashuri ajyana n’ibitabo umuntu aba yaranditse. Icyo gihe si ngombwa
kubishyiraho keretse iyo bigira icyo byongera ku kizere umuntu ashobora
kugirirwa n’abo ashyikiriza umwirondoro.

4. Uburambe

Uburambe mu kazi ni igice cyo kwitonderwa. Aha ni ho uwandika umwirondoro
aba agomba kwereka uwo yandikiye icyo azi gukora n’igihe amaze agikora.
Iyo yakoze mu myanya myinshi, ayishyiraho ahereye ku wa nyuma aherukaho
agenda agaragaza igihe yagiye ayimaraho. Hari igihe umuntu aba yarakoze
iyimenyerezamwuga ni ngombwa ko abishyiraho cyanecyane iyo ataramara
igihe kinini akora cyangwa se ari bwo bwa mbere yatse akazi. Ibyo bishobora
kumwongerera amahirwe imbere y’uwo aha umwirondoro.

5. Ubundi bumenyi

Kumenya ibintu byinshi nta cyo bitwaye kuko ibyo umuntu azi byose bishobora
kumugirira akamaro mu buzima bwe bwa buri munsi. Iyo rero umuntu azi ubundi
bumenyi ntashidikanya kubigaragaza ku mwirondoro we cyanecyane iyo bifitanye
isano n’akazi asaba. Urugero: Kuba azi mudasobwa, kuba afite uruhushya rwo
gutwara ikinyabiziga.

6. Indimi

Hari igihe umwanya umuntu ashaka uba usaba kumenya indimi z’amahanga. Ni
ngombwa rero ko uwandika umwirondoro ashyiramo indimi zose azi. Mu kazi
ako ari ko kose kumenya indimi z’amahanga byongerera amahirwe ugasaba. Ukora
umwirondoro agaragaza urwego aziho urwo rurimi atabeshya (nduzi neza cyane,
nduzi neza, nduzi bihagije, biciriritse) kuko kubeshya byamugiraho ingaruka mu
gihe k’ikizamini k’ibiganiro.

288

7. Ibyo ukunda

Umuntu ntabaho akora akazi ashinzwe gusa. Na nyuma y’akazi ubuzima
burakomeza. Ibyo umuntu akunda rero biza nyuma y’akazi. Bigizwe n’ibyo umuntu
akora kandi bimushimisha. Ariko nanone ukora umwirondoro ntiyiyibagize ko
ibimushimisha bishobora kumubera imbogamizi mu kubona umwanya yifuza.
Nk’urugero niba ari umuntu ukunda kumva indirimbo kuri radiyo, bikaba
byerekana ko ari umuntu ukunze kuba ari wenyine ko kubana n’abandi byamugora,
mu gihe umuntu ukunda gukina umupira aba agaragaza ko abana n’abandi neza
ko no mu kazi byagenda bityo.

8. Abantu bamuzi cyangwa abahamya

Iyi ngingo y’abantu bazi nyiri umwirondoro si ngombwa buri gihe. Ariko
hari ababisaba mu mwirondoro bikaba ngombwa ko ijyamo. Abantu bakunze
gukenerwa si abaturanyi bawe bakomeye cyangwa se bene wanyu bakomeye.
Abazi umuntu baba bakenewe ni abarimu bamwigishije cyangwa abakoresha
bamukoresheje kuko ukeneye umwirondoro wawe aba ashobora kubabaza ku
bijyanye n’ubumenyi ufite cyangwa se ubushobozi n’imyitwarire byawe mu kazi.

9. Kwemeza ko ibyo uvuze ari ukuri no gushyiraho umukono

Iki ni cyo gice gisoza umwirondoro. Nyiri ukuwandika agomba gusoza yemeza
ko amakuru yatanze ari ukuri ko anashobora kugenzurwa. Hanyuma agashyiraho
itariki n’umukono we.
Ibi ni byo by’ingenzi biba bikubiye mu mwirondoro. Cyakora ntibibujijwe ko hari
andi makuru yakongeramo igihe abona ko hari amahirwe yamwongerera kugira
ngo abone akazi yasabaga.

Umukoro

Nyuma yo kumurika ibyavuye mumatsinda no kubinonosora bafatanije n’ umwarimu,
abaha umukoro bakazawukorera mu rugo ugakosorwa mbere yo gutangira isomo
rizakurikiraho.
Urugero rw’umukoro n’uko wakosorwa:
“Ubu urangije mu mwaka wa gatandatu w’amashuri yisumbuye. Andika umwirondoro
wawe ukurikije ingingo ziri hejuru aha ufitiye amakuru.”
Umwarimu azareba niba abanyeshuri bubahirije uturango n’imbata by’umwirondoro
afatanye na bo kujora imwe mu myirondoro yanditswe n’abanyeshuri.

289

Intera ya gatanu: Amatangazo n’ubutumire
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri urupuro rwa 206)

Intego zihariye:
Ahereye ku bushakashatsi yakoze, nyuma y’iyi ntera umunyeshuri arashobora:
– Gusobanura uturango tw’itangazo n’ubutumire.
– Kurondora no gusobanura ubwoko bw’amatangazo bunyuranye.
– Kwandika itangazo n’ubutumire.
Imfashanyigisho: igitabo cy’umunyeshuri, igitabo cy’umwarimu, amatangazo
anyuranye n’ubutumire bunyuranye.

Imitangire y’isomo
1. Ivumburamatsiko

Umwarimu asaba abanyeshuri kwitegereza amashusho ari mu gitabo cyabo
akayababazaho ibibazo biganisha ku isomo bagiye kwiga.

Urugero rw’ibibazo yababaza:
a) Ni iki mubona kuri aya mashusho? Turahabona abanyeshuri babiri b’abahungu
bari gusoma itangazo rimanitse. Turahabona kandi umukobwa ureba ubutumire
arimo kubusoma.
b) Ku bwanyu mwumva amatangazo aba agamije iki ? Aba agamije kumenyesha
ikintu runaka abandi bantu.
Umwarimu araboneraho kubwira abanyeshuri ko bagiye kwiga amatangazo
n’ubutumire abasabe kurambura ibitabo byabo ahari amatangazo n‘ubutumire.

2. Isesengura

Muri iki gice umwarimu arasaba abanyeshuri kujya mu matsinda anyuranye
amatsinda amwe ayasabe gusoma itangazo rigaragara mu bitabo byabo, andi
ayasabe gusoma ubutumire bitegereza imiterere yabyo. Nyuma y’icyo gikorwa
arabasaba abanyeshuri bagize buri tsinda gutahura imiterere n’uturango
tw’amatangazo n’ubutumire bakora ubushakashatsi bwimbitse.
Amatsinda yahaye gukora ku matangazo arabanza amurike ibyo yagezeho
narangiza amatsinda yahaye gukora ku butumire arakurikiraho. Umwarimu
arafatanya n’andi matsinda kunonosora ibyavuye mu bushakashatsi bw’ayo
matsinda byandikwe ku kibaho no mu makaye yabo.

290

Urugero rw’ibyava mu matsinda:

A. Itangazo

1. Inshoza y’itangazo

Igihe cyose ufite icyo ushaka kugeza ku bandi ukimenyesha ugicishije mu itangazo.
Iyo banki zambuwe cyangwa se ibindi bigo biciriritse by’imari bihemukiwe na ba
bihemu bakambura inguzanyo, aba ba bihemu bashyikirizwa inkiko batsindwa
ibyabo bigatezwa cyamunara. Iyo hari imitungo izagurishwa abahesha b’inkiko
b’umwuga batanga amatangazo bahamagarira abaturage kuzaza kwigurira. Bibaho
kandi ko iyo umuryango watakaje umuntu, uhitisha itangazo mu bitangazamakuru
na radiyo bahamagarira abantu gutabara umuryango wagize ibyago. Itangazo ni
inyandiko irimo ubutumwa bamanika ahantu cyangwa bugaca mu kinyamakuru
kugira ngo bumenyekane hagamijwe kwamamaza, kurangisha kumenyesha
n’ibindi.

2. Uturango tw’itangazo

Mu itangazo hagomba kubonekamo ibi bikurikira:
– Umutwe w’itangazo.
– Utanze itangazo.
– Uwo rigenewe.
– Ahantu igikorwa rimenyesha kiri, cyabereye cyangwa kizabera.
– Itariki igikorwa rimenyesha cyabereyeho cyangwa kizabera.

3. Ubwoko bw’amatangazo

Amatangazo yo kubika: Ni amatangazo atabaza agamije kumenyesha abantu
ko hari umuntu witabye Imana akanavuga igihe azashyingurirwa.
Amatangazo yo kumenyesha: Ni amatangazo amenyesha abayumva amakuru
atandukanye nk’inama, akazi, isoko ry’ibintu, cyamunara.
Amatangazo yo kwamamaza: Ni amatangazo atangwa agamije kwamamaza
ibikorwa by’umuntu ku giti ke, by’ishyirahamwe, by’inganda, amashuri, kugira
ngo bimenyekane bibone ababigana mu buryo bwo kubiteza imbere.
Amatangazo yo kurangisha: Ni amatangazo atangwa igihe umuntu yatakaje
ikintu, yabuze umuntu, kugira ngo ababimuboneye babimuhe cyangwa yatoye
ibintu kugira ngo nyirabyo abashe kubibona.

Umukoro

Abanyeshuri yahaye gukora ku matangazo nibarangiza kumurika ibyo bagezeho no
gukorerwa ubugororangingo bafatanyije na mwarimu, arabaha umukoro bazakorera
mu rugo uzakosorerwe hamwe mu ishuri mbere yo gutangira irindi somo.

291

Urugero rw’umukoro n’uko wakosorwa:
Andika amatangazo akurikira wubahiriza imiterere yayo:
– Itangazo ryo kubika.
– Itangazo ryo kumenyesha.
Umwarimu azareba niba ayo matangazo yanditswe hakurikije uturango twayo
n’insanganyamatsiko zatanzwe. Afatemo amwe mu matangazo yakozwe (iryujuje
ibisabwa n’irindi ritujuje ibisabwa) bayajorere hamwe mu ishuri.

B. Ubutumire
1) Inshoza y’ubutumire

Ubutumire ni inyandiko ngufi itumira umuntu cyangwa abantu kwitabira umunsi
mukuru runaka. Bene izi nyandiko twazigereranya n’amabaruwa y’ubucuti nubwo
zo zidakurikiza imiterere y’ayo mabaruwa. Ubutumire bukoreshwa mu minsi
mikuru inyuranye nko gushyingirwa, kubatirisha, kwizihiza isabukuru runaka,
gutaha igikorwa runaka, gusangira ku meza, kwishimira kugera ku gikorwa runaka
nko gufata impamyabumenyi n’ibindi.

2) Uturango tw’ubutumire

Ubutumire burangwa n’imiterere yabwo yo kuba hagaragaramo ibintu by’ingenzi
bikurikira:
– Umutwe w’ubutumire.
– Amazina y’utumira.
– Utumirwa.
– Igikorwa umutumiramo.
– Aho igikorwa kizabera.
– Umunsi n’isaha kizaberaho.
– Aho utumira abarizwa.

Umukoro

Abanyeshuri yahaye gukora ku butumire nibarangiza kumurika ibyo bagezeho no
gukorerwa ubugororangingo bafatanije na mwarimu, arabaha umukoro bazakorera
mu rugo uzakosorerwe hamwe mu ishuri mbere yo gutangira irindi somo.
Urugero rw’umukoro n’uko wakosorwa:
“Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umuyobozi w’ishuri ryisumbuye wigamo, wandike ubutumire
bw’umuhango wo gutanga impamyabumenyi ku banyeshuri barangiza no kwizihiza
isabukuru y’imyaka 25 ikigo kimaze gishinzwe.”
Umwarimu azareba niba ubwo butumire babwanditse bakurikije uturango twabwo
n’insanganyamatsiko yatanzwe. Afatemo bumwe mu butumire bwanditswe (ubwujuje
ibisabwa n’ubundi butujuje ibisabwa) babujorere hamwe mu ishuri.

292

Inshamake y’ibikubiye muri uyu mutwe wa cumi

Umwarimu abaza abanyeshuri ibibazo binyuranye bituma bashobora kuvuga
iby’ingenzi bikubiye mu mutwe wa cumi. Ibyo bibazo yababaza bishobora gutuma
batanga inshamake ikurikira:
– Ibiyobyabwenge bigira ingaruka zinyuranye ku muntu ubikoresha, ku muryango
we ndetse no ku iterambere muri rusange, ko ngomba kurwanya ikoreshwa
ryabyo no gushishikariza bagenzi bange kutabikoresha ndetse no kubirwanya.
– Uwandika ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi akoresha amagambo yabugenewe akirinda
kurondogora, gusuhuza cyangwa gusezera n’andi marangamutima. Ibaruwa
y’ubutegetsi iba ari ngufi kandi ikagira amagambo yabugenewe agomba
kuyitangira no kuyisoza. Urugero rwayitangira: (Bwana/Madamu Muyobozi/
Nyakubahwa Minisitiri… Urugero rwayisoza: Mu gihe ngitegereje igisubizo
cyanyu, nyakubahwa, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mu gihe ntegerezanyije ikizere,
mbaye mbashimiye/ Mbaye mbashimiye Nyakubahwa/ Bwana / Madamu/
Madamazera…)
– Umwirondoro ni ibiranga umuntu: amazina y’ababyeyi be, aho atuye, ibyo yize,
ibyo yakoze, ibyo akora, ibyo akunda ndetse n’ibyo azi.
– Itangazo ni ubutumwa bugenerwa abantu bufite icyo bubamenyesha cyangwa
bubahamagarira. Muri ubwo butumwa hakaba hagaragaramo ubutanze, uwo
abugeneye, ahantu n’igihe.
– Ubutumire ni inyandiko ngufi itumira umuntu cyangwa abantu kwitabira
umunsi mukuru runaka, bukaba burangwa no kugaragaramo amazina y’utumira,
utumirwa, igikorwa cyabaye amutumiramo, aho kizabera, umunsi n’isaha
kizaberaho n’aho utumira abarizwa.

Ibibazo n’ibisubizo by’isuzuma rusange risoza umutwe wa cumi
(Igitabo cy’umunyeshuri)

Ibigenderwaho mu isuzuma:

– Ubushobozi bwo gusubiza ibibazo ku mwandiko yasomye.
– Ubushobozi bwo kwandika umwirondoro, ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi, amatangazo
n’ubutumire.

Uyu mwitozo ukorwa n’umunyeshuri ku giti ke nyuma yawo umwarimu aramukosora
akareba ibibazo byihariye buri munyeshuri afite kugira ngo arebe uko yamufasha.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva no gusesengura umwandiko

1. Ni nde uvugwa muri uyu mwandiko? Aravugwaho iki? Iyo umurebye
ubona arangwa n’iki? Kubera iki? Ni Muvara. Aravugwaho gukoresha
ibiyobyabwenge. Arangwa no kuba atukuye amaso no kugenda yivugisha
mu nzira. Ni ukubera kunywa ibiyobyabwenge.

293

2. Ni izihe ngaruka umunyeshuri ukoresha ibiyobyabwenge agira?
		 Ingaruka agira ni uguta ishuri, gusiba kenshi mu ishuri bigatuma atsindwa,
gutukura amaso, kudasinzira igihe atabikoresheje, gukoresha imvugo
nyandagazi, gushira isoni.
3. Ni iki gituma abanyeshuri bakoresha ibiyobyabwenge? Ni uko babeshywa
ko iyo babikoresheje bituma batinyuka, bagasubiza mu ishuri bashize
amanga kandi ko ngo binatera akanyabugabo.
4. Ni izihe ngamba zivugwa mu mwandiko zo kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge
mu rubyiruko no mashuri by’umwihariko?
		 - Abaturanyi n’ababyeyi bagomba gukurikirana imyigire y’abana babo
ku ishuri, bakamenya igituma batiga neza kandi bakagenzura niba
amafaranga yose abana babo babasaba bavuga ko bayatumwe ku ishuri
biba ari byo koko.
		 - Buri mubyeyi agomba guhuza urugwiro n’umwana uwo ari we wese
abona ko afite ikibazo akamuganiriza ndetse akanamugira inama.
		 - Abayobozi b’ishuri bakurikirana imyigire y’abanyeshuri biga bataha
iwabo bakagenzura ibyo bashobora kuzana ku ishuri ikindi kandi
bakabaza ababyeyi impamvu abana babo bataboneka buri munsi ku
ishuri iyo hari abo babonaho iyo ngeso yo gusiba kenshi. Abayobozi
b’ishuri kandi bagomba gushishikariza buri munyeshuri kugaragaza
bagenzi be bakoresha ibiyobyabwenge cyangwa se abakekwaho
kubikoresha kugira ngo bagirwe inama.
		 - Ni ngombwa gufatanya n’ubuyobozi bwa Leta bakagenzura abacuruza
ibiyobyabwenge babiha abanyeshuri bakabashyikiriza inzego
zibishinzwe.
5. Ingingo z’ingenzi ziri mu mwandiko
		- Imiterere ya Muvara.
		 - Muvara ahura n’umubyeyi.
		 - Muvara aganira n’umubyeyi.
		 - Umubyeyi agira inama Muvara.
		 - Muvara yiyemeza kureka ibiyobyabwenge.
		 Ingingo z’ingereka ziri mu mwandiko
		 - Ibiyobyabwenge abanyeshuri bakoresha.
		 - Igihe abanyeshuri bafatira ibiyobyabwenge.
		 - Aho abanyeshuri bakura ibiyobyabwenge n’uko babona amafaranga yo
kubigura.
		 - Impamvu ituma bamwe mu banyeshuri bakoresha ibiyobyabwenge.
		- Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge.
		 - Ingamba zo kwurwanya ibiyobyabwenge mu mashuri.

294

6. Inyigisho ivuye mu mwandiko
		 Kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge no gushishikariza abandi kubirwanya.
7. Gereranya ubuzima ubamo n’ibivugwa mu mwandiko ugeze ku bandi
inyigisho ukuye muri uyu mwandiko.
		Aha abanyeshuri batanga ibisubizo binyuranye.
8. Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge mu muryango nyarwanda:
		 Birica, bitera ubukene mu miryango bikanadindiza iterambere
9. Ibyinshi mu biyobyabwenge ni ibinyobwa biba bitujuje ubuziranenge.
Hakorwa iki kugira ngo ubinywa asobanukirwe ibijyanye
n’ubuziranenge?
		- Icyakorwa ni uko umwarimu n’ababyeyi bajya baganiriza kenshi abana
n’abanyeshuri by’umwihariko ibijyanye n’ubuziranenge.
		- Abakozi b’Ikigo k’Igihugu Gishinzwe Ubuziranenge bakwiye kujya
bafata umwanya wo kuzenguruka mu mashuri babasobanurira ibijyanye
n’ibinyobwa n’ibiribwa byujuje n’ibitujuje ubuziranenge.

II. Inyunguramagambo:

a) Shaka ibisobanuro by’amagambo akurikira ari mu mwandiko:
1. Agatsibanzira: Inzira nto iri hagati y’imirima abagenzi bacamo bajya
guhinga cyangwa bajya mu ngo zabo.
		2. Atumagura (itabi): anywa itabi asohora buri kanya umwotsi w’itabi.
		3. Impuzankano: Ni imyenda isa iba iranga abantu runaka; abanyeshuri,
abasirikare n’abandi.
		4. Mu maswingi: ni imvugo nyandagazi isobanura kuba mu byishimo
byatewe no gusinda cyangwa gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge.
		5. Kubatwa n’ibiyobyabwenge: kwibasirwa n’ibiyobyabwenge, gukoresha
ibiyobyabwenge buri gihe.
		6. Imvugo nyandagazi: imvugo y’urukozasoni idahesha agaciro
uyikoresha.
		7. Gucururuka: gutuza.
		8. Duhuza urugwiro: turaganira neza.
		9. Babirindutse: basinze, bazengereye, batukuye amaso.
		10. Utubure: udufungo tw’urumogi tuba tuzingiye mu makoma yumye
cyangwa mu bipapuro bya sima.
		11. Siriduwire: Inzoga imeze nka kanyanga ikorerwa mu nganda iba iri
mu gacupa gato.

295

B. Soma, uhuze ijambo rivuye mu mwandiko n’igisobanuro cyaryo
ukoresheje akambi.
Gusabana

Gusangira

Agatsibanzira

Impuzankano

Inzira nto iri hagati y’imirima
abagenzi bacamo bajya guhinga
cyangwa bajya mu ngo zabo.

Guhuza urugwiro
Akayira gato imodoka zicamo.
Ni imyenda isa iba iranga abantu
runaka; abanyeshuri, abasirikare...

III.Umwitozo wo guhanga

1) Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umunyeshuri urangije amashuri yisumbuye mu ishami
wiga, maze wandike ibaruwa isaba akazi kajyanye n’ibyo wize. Ku mugereka
w’ibaruwa wanditse ushyireho umwirondoro wawe.

		Aha umwarimu areba niba ibaruwa umunyeshuri yanditse ijyanye
n’amabwiriza yahawe kandi ko yubahirije uturango tw’ibaruwa y’uburegetsi
ndetse n’imyandikire yemewe y’Ikinyarwanda.
2) Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umubyeyi maze wandikire inshuti yawe uyitumira mu
bukwe bw’umukobwa /umuhungu wawe ugiye gushyingirwa.
		Aha umwarimu areba niba ubutumire bwubahirije uturango twabwo nk’uko
uru rugero rukurikira rubigaragaza.

Imyitozo nzamurabushobozi
–
–

Umwarimu atahura ikigero cy’ubushobozi bwa buri munyeshuri ahereye ku
manota bagize mu isuzuma risoza umutwe akabashyira mu matsinda akurikije
ingorane bafite.
Umwarimu abaha imyitozo ijyanye n’ibyiciro byabo bakayikorera hamwe mu
matsinda. Ku badafite ibibazo, umwarimu abavanga muri ya matsinda y’abafite
ibibazo kugira ngo bafashe abandi gusobanukirwa neza n’imyitozo bakora
cyangwa se akabasaba gukora imyitozo nyagurabushobozi. Iyi myitozo umwarimu
ayitegura ashingiye ku bibazo abanyeshuri be bafite yibanda aho abanyeshuri be
bagaragaje ubushobozi buke.

Urugero rw’imyitozo yabaha:
a) Ku bafite ikibazo ku kumva umwandiko umwarimu abategurira ibibazo byimbitse
ku nsanganyamatsiko y’ibiyobyabwenge.
b) Ku bafite ibibazo ku ibaruwa n’umwirondoro umwarimu yongera kubasaba gukora
imyirondoro yabo no kwandika ibaruwa runaka y’ubutegetsi (isaba ibisobanuro
cyangwa isaba akazi)
c) Ku bafite ikibazo ku matangazo n’ubutumire umwarimu abasaba kwandika
ubutumire n’amatangazo anyuranye.
